# ISSUE #3 PREVIEW FIX:
# Database URL now loaded from environment variable.
# No credentials hardcoded in source code.
DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")

if not DATABASE_URL:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "DATABASE_URL environment variable is not set. "
        "Server cannot start. Please set it in your .env file."
    )

engine = create_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    pool_size=10,
    max_overflow=20,
    pool_recycle=1800,
    pool_pre_ping=True
)

SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)

Base = declarative_base()


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()import os
import logging
import base64
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers import Cipher, algorithms, modes
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import padding
from cryptography.hazmat.backends import default_backend
from dotenv import load_dotenv

# Load environment variables
load_dotenv()

# -----------------------------------------------
# ISSUE #2 FIX:
# 1. No fallback default key. If not set, server refuses to start.
# 2. Key is validated to be exactly 32 bytes using proper method.
# 3. Replaced manual padding with standard PKCS7 padding.
# 4. Added Base64 validation in decrypt to prevent crashes.
# -----------------------------------------------

SECRET_KEY_STR = os.getenv("ENCRYPTION_SECRET_KEY")

if not SECRET_KEY_STR:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "ENCRYPTION_SECRET_KEY environment variable is not set. "
        "Server cannot start. Please set it in your .env file."
    )

# Validate key length
encoded_key = SECRET_KEY_STR.encode('utf-8')
if len(encoded_key) < 32:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"ENCRYPTION_SECRET_KEY is too short. "
        f"It must be at least 32 bytes. Current length: {len(encoded_key)} bytes."
    )
if len(encoded_key) > 32:
    logging.warning("ENCRYPTION_SECRET_KEY is longer than 32 bytes and will be truncated to 32 bytes.")

# Use exactly 32 bytes — no predictable padding
SECRET_KEY = encoded_key[:32]

IV_LENGTH = 16


def encrypt(plain_text: str) -> str:
    """
    Encrypts a plain text string using AES-256-CBC with PKCS7 padding.
    Returns Base64 encoded string of IV + encrypted data.
    Returns empty string if input is empty or None instead of storing plaintext.
    """
    # ISSUE #10 FIX:
    # Previously returned plain_text unchanged if falsy.
    # Now returns empty string to avoid storing plaintext in DB.
    if not plain_text:
        return ""

    try:
        iv = os.urandom(IV_LENGTH)
        cipher = Cipher(
            algorithms.AES(SECRET_KEY),
            modes.CBC(iv),
            backend=default_backend()
        )
        encryptor = cipher.encryptor()

        # ISSUE #20 FIX:
        # Replaced manual padding with standard PKCS7 padding
        padder = padding.PKCS7(128).padder()
        padded_data = padder.update(plain_text.encode('utf-8')) + padder.finalize()

        encrypted = encryptor.update(padded_data) + encryptor.finalize()

        # Combine iv + encrypted and encode to base64
        result = base64.b64encode(iv + encrypted).decode('utf-8')
        return result

    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Encryption failed: {str(e)}")


def decrypt(encrypted_text: str) -> str:
    """
    Decrypts a Base64 encoded AES-256-CBC encrypted string.
    Returns empty string if input is empty or None.
    Validates Base64 input before decoding to prevent crashes.
    """
    # ISSUE #11 FIX:
    # Added validation before Base64 decoding to prevent unhandled crashes.
    if not encrypted_text:
        return ""

    try:
        # Validate it is proper Base64 before decoding
        try:
            raw = base64.b64decode(encrypted_text, validate=True)
        except Exception:
            raise ValueError("Invalid encrypted data format. Not valid Base64.")

        if len(raw) < IV_LENGTH:
            raise ValueError("Invalid encrypted data. Too short to contain IV.")

        iv = raw[:IV_LENGTH]
        encrypted = raw[IV_LENGTH:]

        cipher = Cipher(
            algorithms.AES(SECRET_KEY),
            modes.CBC(iv),
            backend=default_backend()
        )
        decryptor = cipher.decryptor()
        padded_decrypted = decryptor.update(encrypted) + decryptor.finalize()

        # Remove PKCS7 padding
        unpadder = padding.PKCS7(128).unpadder()
        decrypted = unpadder.update(padded_decrypted) + unpadder.finalize()

        return decrypted.decode('utf-8')

    except ValueError:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Decryption failed: {str(e)}")import firebase_admin
from firebase_admin import credentials, messaging
import os
import json
import logging
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from backend.config.encryption import decrypt

load_dotenv()

def safe_decrypt_fcm_token(encrypted_token: str) -> str:
    """
    Safely decrypts an FCM token. If decryption fails (e.g., token is stored 
    as plain text from an older version), it returns the raw token.
    """
    if not encrypted_token:
        return ""
    try:
        return decrypt(encrypted_token)
    except Exception as e:
        # If it's already plain text, decryption will fail (Invalid Base64 or padding)
        # We return it as-is for legacy support.
        logging.warning(f"Decryption failed for token, treating as plain text: {e}")
        return encrypted_token

_firebase_initialized = False

def initialize_firebase():
    global _firebase_initialized
    if not _firebase_initialized:
        try:
            env_path = os.getenv("FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS_PATH")
            if env_path:
                json_path = os.path.abspath(env_path)
            else:
                json_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'firebase_credentials.json')
                json_path = os.path.abspath(json_path)
            
            if os.path.exists(json_path):
                cred = credentials.Certificate(json_path)
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Firebase credentials not found at: {json_path}")
                
            firebase_admin.initialize_app(cred)
            _firebase_initialized = True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Firebase initialization failed: {e}")
            raise

from backend.models.models import NotificationInbox

def send_push_notification(title: str, body: str, topic: str = "all", db=None):
    try:
        # NotificationInbox is per-user — do not save broadcast push here
        # Per-user inbox is handled separately per route if needed
        pass
                
        initialize_firebase()
        message = messaging.Message(
            notification=messaging.Notification(
                title=title,
                body=body,
            ),
            topic=topic,
        )
        response = messaging.send(message)
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Push notification failed: {e}")
        return None

def send_push_to_token(title: str, body: str, token: str):
    """Sends a notification to a specific FCM token (Security Fix: Now uses stored tokens)"""
    if not token:
        return None
    try:
        initialize_firebase()
        message = messaging.Message(
            notification=messaging.Notification(
                title=title,
                body=body,
            ),
            token=token,
        )
        response = messaging.send(message)
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Push notification failed: {e}")
        return None# Backend Localization Dictionary

def get_msg(key: str, lang: str = "en") -> str:
    """Returns the translated message based on key and language."""
    # Default to English if language not supported or key missing
    if lang not in MESSAGES:
        lang = "en"
    return MESSAGES[lang].get(key, MESSAGES["en"].get(key, key))

# Users Table (Admin & Farmer)
class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    email = Column(String(100), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    role = Column(String(20), nullable=False)
    contact = Column(String(255))  # encrypted
    location = Column(String(500))  # encrypted
    language = Column(String(10), default="en")
    status = Column(String(20), default="active")
    fcm_token = Column(String(255)) # ISSUE 4 FIX: Added for push notifications
    pin_hash = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())

# Token Blacklist Table (ISSUE 2 FIX: Server-side logout)
class TokenBlacklist(Base):
    __tablename__ = "token_blacklist"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    token = Column(String(500), nullable=False, index=True)
    blacklisted_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), index=True)

class RefreshTokenBlacklist(Base):
    __tablename__ = "refresh_token_blacklist"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    token = Column(String(500), nullable=False, index=True)
    blacklisted_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), index=True)

# Password Reset Token (ISSUE 1 FIX)
class PasswordResetToken(Base):
    __tablename__ = "password_reset_tokens"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    email = Column(String(100), nullable=False, index=True)
    token = Column(String(100), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    expires_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    used = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())

# Brute Force Protection (ISSUE 5 FIX: Database-backed)
class LoginAttempt(Base):
    __tablename__ = "login_attempts"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    email = Column(String(100), nullable=False, index=True)
    attempt_time = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
    ip_address = Column(String(45)) # IPv6 support

class AuditLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "audit_logs"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    user_email = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    action = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # CREATE, UPDATE, DELETE
    resource = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # e.g. "Announcement", "CropTracker"
    resource_id = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    details = Column(Text, nullable=True)  # JSON string of changed fields
    ip_address = Column(String(45), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), index=True)

class NotificationInbox(Base):
    __tablename__ = "notification_inbox"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    title = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    body = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    topic = Column(String(50), default="all")
    is_read = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), index=True)

# Announcements Table

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from backend.config.database import get_db

from backend.models.models import Announcement, User, AuditLog

from starlette.requests import Request


from pydantic import BaseModel, field_validator
from typing import Optional

router = APIRouter()

class AnnouncementRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str
    message: str
    category: Optional[str] = "General"
    target_audience: Optional[str] = "all"

    @field_validator('title')
    def title_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Title cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('message')
    def message_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Message cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()


@router.get("/")
def get_announcements(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100), # ISSUE 7 FIX: Capped pagination
    category: Optional[str] = None,
    target_audience: Optional[str] = None
):
    try:
        query = db.query(Announcement)
        if category:
            query = query.filter(Announcement.category == category)
        if target_audience:
            query = query.filter(Announcement.target_audience == target_audience)
            
        total = query.count()
        announcements = query.order_by(Announcement.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
        return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": announcements}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch announcements: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/{id}")
def get_announcement(
    id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        announcement = db.query(Announcement).filter(Announcement.id == id).first()
        if not announcement:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Announcement not found")
        return announcement
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch announcement: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/")
def create_announcement(
    request: AnnouncementRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        new_announcement = Announcement(
            title=request.title,
            message=request.message,
            category=request.category,
            target_audience=request.target_audience,
            created_by=current_admin.id
        )
        db.add(new_announcement)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(new_announcement)

        # Send FCM push to every farmer with a registered token (FIX: was broken topic-based)
        notif_title = f"\U0001F4E2 {request.title}"
        notif_body = request.message
        farmers = db.query(User).filter(User.fcm_token != None, User.fcm_token != "").all()
        sent = 0
        for farmer in farmers:
            try:
                raw_token = safe_decrypt_fcm_token(farmer.fcm_token)  # FIX: token might be encrypted or plain
                if not raw_token:
                    continue
                result = send_push_to_token(
                    title=notif_title,
                    body=notif_body,
                    token=raw_token
                )
                if result:
                    sent += 1
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Push to farmer {farmer.id} failed: {e}")
        print(f"Announcement push sent to {sent}/{len(farmers)} farmers")

        log_action(db, current_admin, "CREATE", "Announcement", new_announcement.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": f"Announcement created. Notified {sent} farmer(s).", "id": new_announcement.id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to create announcement: {str(e)}")


@router.put("/{id}")
def update_announcement(
    id: int,
    request: AnnouncementRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        announcement = db.query(Announcement).filter(Announcement.id == id).first()
        if not announcement:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Announcement not found")

        announcement.title = request.title
        announcement.message = request.message
        announcement.category = request.category
        announcement.target_audience = request.target_audience
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "UPDATE", "Announcement", announcement.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Announcement updated successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update announcement: {str(e)}")


@router.delete("/{id}")
def delete_announcement(
    id: int,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        announcement = db.query(Announcement).filter(Announcement.id == id).first()
        if not announcement:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Announcement not found")

        db.delete(announcement)
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "DELETE", "Announcement", id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Announcement deleted successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to delete announcement: {str(e)}")from typing import Optional

from backend.models.models import User, LoginAttempt, TokenBlacklist, RefreshTokenBlacklist, PasswordResetToken, AuditLog, NotificationInbox, OTPVerification
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, field_validator
import os
import secrets
import logging
import smtplib
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

router = APIRouter()
limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
_last_blacklist_cleanup = None  # Throttling variable for token cleanup

def log_action(db, user, action, resource, resource_id=None, details=None, request=None):
    try:
        entry = AuditLog(
            user_id=user.id if user else None,
            user_email=user.email if user else None,
            action=action,
            resource=resource,
            resource_id=resource_id,
            details=details,
            ip_address=request.client.host if request and hasattr(request, 'client') else None
        )
        db.add(entry)
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass  # Never let audit logging break main functionality

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")

SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY")
if not SECRET_KEY:
    raise RuntimeError("JWT_SECRET_KEY environment variable is not set. Server cannot start.")

ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 15  # ISSUE 3 FIX: Access token expires in 15 mins
REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS = 7    # ISSUE 3 FIX: Refresh token expires in 7 days


class RegisterRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    email: EmailStr
    password: str
    contact: str = ""
    location: str = ""

    @field_validator('name')
    def name_must_not_be_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Name cannot be empty')
        if len(v) < 2:
            raise ValueError('Name must be at least 2 characters')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('password')
    def password_must_be_strong(cls, v):
        if len(v) < 8:
            raise ValueError('Password must be at least 8 characters')
        if not any(c.isupper() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 uppercase letter')
        if not any(c.islower() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 lowercase letter')
        if not any(c.isdigit() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 number')
        if not any(c in '!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{}|;:,.<>?' for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 special character')
        return v




class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str


class AdminLoginRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str


class ProfileUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    contact: str = ""
    location: str = ""

    @field_validator('name')
    def name_must_not_be_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Name cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()


class PasswordChangeRequest(BaseModel):
    current_password: str
    new_password: str

    @field_validator('new_password')
    def password_must_be_strong(cls, v):
        if len(v) < 8:
            raise ValueError('Password must be at least 8 characters')
        if not any(c.isupper() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 uppercase letter')
        if not any(c.islower() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 lowercase letter')
        if not any(c.isdigit() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password contain at least 1 number')
        if not any(c in '!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{}|;:,.<>?' for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 special character')
        return v


class ForgotPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr


class ResetPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    token: str
    new_password: str

    @field_validator('new_password')
    def password_must_be_strong(cls, v):
        if len(v) < 8:
            raise ValueError('Password must be at least 8 characters')
        if not any(c.isupper() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 uppercase letter')
        if not any(c.islower() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 lowercase letter')
        if not any(c.isdigit() for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password contain at least 1 number')
        if not any(c in '!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{}|;:,.<>?' for c in v):
            raise ValueError('Password must contain at least 1 special character')
        return v


class RefreshRequest(BaseModel):
    refresh_token: str


class FCMTokenRequest(BaseModel):
    fcm_token: str


class OTPVerifyRequest(BaseModel):
    otp_code: str


def create_access_token(data: dict):
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    to_encode = data.copy()
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire, "type": "access"})
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)


def create_refresh_token(data: dict):
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS)
    to_encode = data.copy()
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire, "type": "refresh"})
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)


def verify_token(authorization: str = Header(None), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    if not authorization:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authorization header missing")
    parts = authorization.split()
    if len(parts) != 2:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid authorization header format")
    scheme, token = parts
    if scheme.lower() != "bearer":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid authentication scheme")
    is_blacklisted = db.query(TokenBlacklist).filter(TokenBlacklist.token == token).first()
    if is_blacklisted:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Token has been invalidated")
    # TOKEN BLACKLIST CLEANUP FIX (Throttled to once every 5 minutes)
    global _last_blacklist_cleanup
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if _last_blacklist_cleanup is None or (now - _last_blacklist_cleanup).total_seconds() > 300:
        cutoff = now - timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
        db.query(TokenBlacklist).filter(TokenBlacklist.blacklisted_at < cutoff).delete()
        db.commit()
        _last_blacklist_cleanup = now
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        if payload.get("type") != "access":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token type")
        return payload
    except JWTError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired token")


def get_current_user(db: Session = Depends(get_db), token_data: dict = Depends(verify_token)) -> User:
    email = token_data.get("sub")
    if not email:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token payload")
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == email).first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="User not found")
    return user


def safe_decrypt(data: str) -> str:
    if not data:
        return ""
    try:
        return decrypt(data)
    except Exception:
        return ""  # Return empty string instead of exposing raw/corrupted data


def get_current_admin(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), x_language: str = Header("en")) -> User:
    if current_user.role != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=get_msg("access_denied_admin", x_language))
    return current_user


@router.post("/register")
def register(request: RegisterRequest, fastapi_req: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db), x_language: str = Header("en")):
    try:
        lang = x_language
        existing_user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == request.email).first()
        if existing_user:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=get_msg("email_registered", lang))
        hashed_password = pwd_context.hash(request.password)
        new_user = User(
            name=request.name,
            email=request.email,
            password=hashed_password,
            role="farmer",
            contact=encrypt(request.contact),
            location=encrypt(request.location)
        )
        db.add(new_user)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(new_user)
        log_action(db, new_user, "CREATE", "User", new_user.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "User registered successfully", "user_id": new_user.id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"{get_msg('generic_error', x_language)}: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/login")
@limiter.limit("5 per minute")  # ISSUE 2 FIX: Login limit
def login(request: Request, data: LoginRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), x_language: str = Header("en")):
    try:
        lang = x_language
        # ISSUE 5 FIX: Database-backed brute force protection
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        five_mins_ago = now - timedelta(minutes=5)
        failed_attempts = db.query(LoginAttempt).filter(
            LoginAttempt.email == data.email,
            LoginAttempt.attempt_time >= five_mins_ago
        ).count()
        if failed_attempts >= 5:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Too many failed attempts. Account locked for 5 minutes.")
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == data.email).first()
        if not user or not pwd_context.verify(data.password, user.password):
            # Record failed attempt
            attempt = LoginAttempt(email=data.email, ip_address=request.client.host)
            db.add(attempt)
            db.commit()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=get_msg("invalid_credentials", lang))
        if user.status != "active":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Account is deactivated. Contact your administrator.")
        # SUCCESS: Create both Access and Refresh tokens
        access_token = create_access_token({"sub": user.email, "role": user.role, "id": user.id})
        refresh_token = create_refresh_token({"sub": user.email})
        # PURGE old login attempts (records older than 5 minutes)
        db.query(LoginAttempt).filter(
            LoginAttempt.attempt_time < five_mins_ago
        ).delete()
        db.commit()
        return {
            "access_token": access_token,
            "refresh_token": refresh_token,
            "token_type": "bearer",
            "role": user.role,
            "name": user.name
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Login failed: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/admin/login")
@limiter.limit("5 per minute")  # ISSUE 2 FIX: Login limit
def admin_login(request: Request, data: AdminLoginRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), x_language: str = Header("en")):
    try:
        lang = x_language
        # ISSUE 5 FIX: Database-backed brute force protection
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        five_mins_ago = now - timedelta(minutes=5)
        failed_attempts = db.query(LoginAttempt).filter(
            LoginAttempt.email == data.email,
            LoginAttempt.attempt_time >= five_mins_ago
        ).count()
        if failed_attempts >= 5:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Too many login attempts. Try again in 5 minutes.")
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == data.email).first()
        if not user or not pwd_context.verify(data.password, user.password):
            # Record failed attempt
            attempt = LoginAttempt(email=data.email, ip_address=request.client.host)
            db.add(attempt)
            db.commit()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=get_msg("invalid_credentials", lang))
        if user.status != "active":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Account is deactivated. Contact your administrator.")
        if user.role != "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=get_msg("access_denied_portal", lang))
        access_token = create_access_token({"sub": user.email, "role": user.role, "id": user.id})
        refresh_token = create_refresh_token({"sub": user.email})
        # PURGE old login attempts (records older than 5 minutes)
        db.query(LoginAttempt).filter(
            LoginAttempt.attempt_time < five_mins_ago
        ).delete()
        db.commit()
        return {
            "access_token": access_token,
            "refresh_token": refresh_token,
            "token_type": "bearer",
            "role": user.role,
            "name": user.name
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Login failed: {str(e)}")


class LogoutRequest(BaseModel):
    refresh_token: Optional[str] = None

@router.post("/logout")
def logout(
    data: LogoutRequest,
    authorization: str = Header(None),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    if not authorization:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authorization header missing")
    parts = authorization.split()
    if len(parts) == 2 and parts[0].lower() == "bearer":
        token = parts[1]
        # Blacklist access token
        cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
        db.query(TokenBlacklist).filter(TokenBlacklist.blacklisted_at < cutoff).delete()
        db.commit()
        existing = db.query(TokenBlacklist).filter(TokenBlacklist.token == token).first()
        if not existing:
            db.add(TokenBlacklist(token=token))
            db.commit()
    # Blacklist refresh token if provided
    if data.refresh_token:
        refresh_cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS)
        db.query(RefreshTokenBlacklist).filter(
            RefreshTokenBlacklist.blacklisted_at < refresh_cutoff
        ).delete()
        db.commit()
        existing_refresh = db.query(RefreshTokenBlacklist).filter(RefreshTokenBlacklist.token == data.refresh_token).first()
        if not existing_refresh:
            db.add(RefreshTokenBlacklist(token=data.refresh_token))
            db.commit()
    return {"message": "Logged out successfully"}


@router.post("/refresh")  # ISSUE 3 FIX: Refresh token endpoint
def refresh_access_token(data: RefreshRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    # 1. Check if refresh token is blacklisted
    is_blacklisted = db.query(RefreshTokenBlacklist).filter(
        RefreshTokenBlacklist.token == data.refresh_token
    ).first()
    if is_blacklisted:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Refresh token has been invalidated")
    
    try:
        # 2. Decode the refresh token
        payload = jwt.decode(data.refresh_token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        if payload.get("type") != "refresh":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid refresh token")
        email = payload.get("sub")
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == email).first()
        if not user:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="User not found")
        
        # 3. Blacklist the old refresh token (with duplicate check)
        existing_refresh = db.query(RefreshTokenBlacklist).filter(
            RefreshTokenBlacklist.token == data.refresh_token
        ).first()
        if not existing_refresh:
            db.add(RefreshTokenBlacklist(token=data.refresh_token))
            db.commit()
            
        # 4. Issue new access token and refresh token
        new_access_token = create_access_token({"sub": user.email, "role": user.role, "id": user.id})
        new_refresh_token = create_refresh_token({"sub": user.email, "role": user.role, "id": user.id})
        
        # 5. Return new tokens
        return {
            "access_token": new_access_token,
            "refresh_token": new_refresh_token,
            "token_type": "bearer"
        }
    except JWTError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired refresh token")


@router.put("/fcm-token")  # ISSUE 4 FIX: Save FCM token
def update_fcm_token(data: FCMTokenRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    try:
        current_user.fcm_token = encrypt(data.fcm_token)
        db.commit()
        return {"message": "FCM token updated successfully"}
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update FCM token: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/users")
def get_users(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    x_language: str = Header("en"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_admin),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100),  # ISSUE 7 FIX: Capped pagination
    role: Optional[str] = None,
    status: Optional[str] = None
):
    try:
        query = db.query(User)
        if role:
            query = query.filter(User.role == role)
        if status:
            query = query.filter(User.status == status)
        total = query.count()
        users = query.offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
        data = [
            {
                "id": u.id,
                "name": u.name,
                "email": u.email,
                "role": u.role,
                "contact": safe_decrypt(u.contact),
                "location": safe_decrypt(u.location),
                "language": u.language,
                "status": u.status,
                "created_at": u.created_at
            }
            for u in users
        ]
        return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": data}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch users: {str(e)}")

            "created_at": current_user.created_at
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch profile: {str(e)}")


@router.put("/profile")
def update_profile(
    data: ProfileUpdateRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    x_language: str = Header("en"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        current_user.name = data.name
        current_user.contact = encrypt(data.contact)
        current_user.location = encrypt(data.location)
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_user, "UPDATE", "User", current_user.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": get_msg("profile_update_success", x_language)}
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update profile: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/change-password")
def change_password(
    data: PasswordChangeRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    x_language: str = Header("en"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        lang = x_language
        if not pwd_context.verify(data.current_password, current_user.password):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=get_msg("pass_old_incorrect", lang))
        current_user.password = pwd_context.hash(data.new_password)
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_user, "UPDATE", "UserPassword", current_user.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": get_msg("pass_update_success", lang)}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to change password: {str(e)}")


def send_reset_email(to_email: str, reset_token: str):
    mail_user = os.getenv("MAIL_USERNAME")
    mail_pass = os.getenv("MAIL_PASSWORD")
    if not mail_user or not mail_pass:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").warning("MAIL_USERNAME or MAIL_PASSWORD not set. Email not sent.")
        return
    subject = "AgriAssist - Password Reset"
    body = f"""
Hello,

You requested a password reset for your AgriAssist account.

Your one-time reset token is:
{reset_token}

This token expires in 1 hour. Do NOT share this token with anyone.
AgriAssist staff will never ask for your reset token.

If you did not request this, please ignore this email and your account will remain secure.

- AgriAssist Team
"""
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg["From"] = mail_user
    msg["To"] = to_email
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain"))
    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL("smtp.gmail.com", 465) as server:
            server.login(mail_user, mail_pass)
            server.sendmail(mail_user, to_email, msg.as_string())
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").info(f"Reset email sent to {to_email}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").error(f"Failed to send reset email: {e}")


def send_otp_email(to_email: str, otp_code: str):
    mail_user = os.getenv("MAIL_USERNAME")
    mail_pass = os.getenv("MAIL_PASSWORD")
    if not mail_user or not mail_pass:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").warning("MAIL_USERNAME or MAIL_PASSWORD not set. OTP email not sent.")
        return
    subject = "AgriAssist - One-Time Password (OTP)"
    body = f"""
Hello,

Your One-Time Password (OTP) for AgriAssist login is:
{otp_code}

This code expires in 5 minutes.

If you did not request this, please secure your account.

- AgriAssist Team
"""
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg["From"] = mail_user
    msg["To"] = to_email
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain"))
    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL("smtp.gmail.com", 465) as server:
            server.login(mail_user, mail_pass)
            server.sendmail(mail_user, to_email, msg.as_string())
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").info(f"OTP email sent to {to_email}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").error(f"Failed to send OTP email: {e}")


@router.post("/send-otp")
@limiter.limit("3 per minute")
def send_otp(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    otp = f"{secrets.randbelow(1000000):06d}"
    expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=5)
    
    # Invalidate previous OTPs
    db.query(OTPVerification).filter(OTPVerification.user_id == current_user.id, OTPVerification.used == False).update({OTPVerification.used: True})
    
    otp_entry = OTPVerification(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        otp_code=pwd_context.hash(otp),
        expires_at=expires_at
    )
    db.add(otp_entry)
    db.commit()
    send_otp_email(current_user.email, otp)
    return {"message": "OTP sent"}


@router.post("/verify-otp")
@limiter.limit("5 per minute")
def verify_otp(request: Request, data: OTPVerifyRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    otp_entries = db.query(OTPVerification).filter(
        OTPVerification.user_id == current_user.id,
        OTPVerification.used == False,
        OTPVerification.expires_at >= datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ).all()
    
    def safe_verify(plain, hashed):
        try:
            return pwd_context.verify(plain, hashed)
        except Exception:
            return False

    otp_entry = next((e for e in otp_entries if safe_verify(data.otp_code, e.otp_code)), None)
    
    if not otp_entry:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired OTP")
    
    otp_entry.used = True
    db.commit()
    return {"verified": True}


@router.post("/forgot-password")
@limiter.limit("3 per minute")
def forgot_password(request: Request, data: ForgotPasswordRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == data.email).first()
    if not user:
        # Don't reveal if email exists or not
        return {"message": "If this email exists, a reset link has been sent to your email."}
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1)
    reset_token = PasswordResetToken(email=data.email, token=pwd_context.hash(token), expires_at=expires_at)
    db.add(reset_token)
    db.commit()
    send_reset_email(data.email, token)
    return {"message": "If this email exists, a reset link has been sent to your email."}


@router.post("/reset-password")
@limiter.limit("5 per minute")
def reset_password(request: Request, data: ResetPasswordRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    # Fetch all unused unexpired tokens for that email
    # Since ResetPasswordRequest doesn't have email, we have to look up by token?
    # Wait, the ISSUE 14 says: "fetch all unused unexpired tokens for that email"
    # But ResetPasswordRequest only has {token, new_password}.
    # So we must look up by token hash? No, tokens are unique enough.
    # We'll fetch all unused unexpired tokens and verify.
    tokens = db.query(PasswordResetToken).filter(
        PasswordResetToken.used == False,
        PasswordResetToken.expires_at >= datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ).all()
    
    reset_token = next((t for t in tokens if pwd_context.verify(data.token, t.token)), None)
    
    if not reset_token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired reset token")
        
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == reset_token.email).first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired reset token")
    user.password = pwd_context.hash(data.new_password)
    reset_token.used = True
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "Password reset successfully"}


@router.delete("/users/{user_id}")
def delete_user(
    user_id: int,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
        if not user:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
        if user.role == "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Cannot delete an admin account")
        db.delete(user)
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "DELETE", "User", user_id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "User deleted successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to delete user: {str(e)}")

    status: str,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        if status not in ["active", "inactive"]:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Status must be active or inactive")
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
        if not user:

        user.status = status
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "UPDATE", "UserStatus", user_id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": f"User status updated to {status}"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update user status: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/audit-logs")
def get_audit_logs(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100),

):

    total = query.count()
    logs = query.order_by(AuditLog.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
    return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": logs}

@router.put("/notifications/{id}/read")
def read_notification(
    id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    notification = db.query(NotificationInbox).filter(NotificationInbox.id == id).first()
    if not notification:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Notification not found")
    if notification.user_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorized to read this notification")
    notification.is_read = True
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "Notification marked as read"}


@router.put("/notifications/read-all")
def read_all_notifications(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    db.query(NotificationInbox).filter(NotificationInbox.user_id == current_user.id).update({NotificationInbox.is_read: True})
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "All notifications marked as read"}

class PinSetRequest(BaseModel):
    pin: str

class PinVerifyRequest(BaseModel):
    pin: str

@router.post("/set-pin")
async def set_pin(
    request: PinSetRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    if len(request.pin) < 4 or len(request.pin) > 6:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="PIN must be 4-6 digits")
    current_user.pin_hash = pwd_context.hash(request.pin)
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "PIN set successfully"}

@router.post("/verify-pin")
async def verify_pin(
    request: PinVerifyRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    if not current_user.pin_hash:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No PIN set")
    if not pwd_context.verify(request.pin, current_user.pin_hash):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid PIN")
    return {"status": "PIN verified"}

@router.get("/has-pin")
async def has_pin(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    return {"has_pin": current_user.pin_hash is not None}


def send_reset_pin_email(to_email: str, reset_link: str):
    mail_user = os.getenv("MAIL_USERNAME")
    mail_pass = os.getenv("MAIL_PASSWORD")
    if not mail_user or not mail_pass:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").warning("MAIL_USERNAME or MAIL_PASSWORD not set. Reset PIN email not sent.")
        return
    subject = "AgriAssist - Reset PIN"
    body = f"""
Hello,

You requested a PIN reset for your AgriAssist account.

Please click the link below to reset your PIN:
{reset_link}

This link expires in 15 minutes. Do NOT share this link with anyone.

- AgriAssist Team
"""
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg["From"] = mail_user
    msg["To"] = to_email
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain"))
    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL("smtp.gmail.com", 465) as server:
            server.login(mail_user, mail_pass)
            server.sendmail(mail_user, to_email, msg.as_string())
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").info(f"Reset PIN email sent to {to_email}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("agriassist").error(f"Failed to send reset PIN email: {e}")


class ForgotPinRequest(BaseModel):
    email: str


class ResetPinRequest(BaseModel):
    token: str
    pin: str


@router.post("/forgot-pin")
@limiter.limit("3 per minute")
async def forgot_pin(
    request: Request,
    data: ForgotPinRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == data.email).first()
    if not user or user.role != "farmer":
        # Always return 200 generic message to prevent user enumeration
        return {"message": "If this email exists, a reset link has been sent to your email."}

    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=15)
    reset_token = PasswordResetToken(email=data.email, token=pwd_context.hash(token), expires_at=expires_at)
    db.add(reset_token)
    db.commit()

    # Construct the BASE_URL from request dynamically
    base_url = str(request.base_url).rstrip('/')
    reset_link = f"{base_url}/api/auth/reset-pin?token={token}"

    send_reset_pin_email(data.email, reset_link)
    return {"message": "If this email exists, a reset link has been sent to your email."}


@router.get("/reset-pin")
def get_reset_pin_redirect(token: str, request: Request):
    from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
    base_url = str(request.base_url).rstrip('/')
    # Redirect GET request to static reset-pin.html frontend page
    return RedirectResponse(url=f"{base_url}/frontend/pwa/reset-pin.html?token={token}")


@router.post("/reset-pin")
@limiter.limit("5 per minute")
async def reset_pin(
    request: Request,
    data: ResetPinRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    tokens = db.query(PasswordResetToken).filter(
        PasswordResetToken.used == False,
        PasswordResetToken.expires_at >= datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ).all()

    reset_token = next((t for t in tokens if pwd_context.verify(data.token, t.token)), None)

    if not reset_token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired reset token")

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == reset_token.email).first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired reset token")

    if len(data.pin) < 4 or len(data.pin) > 6 or not data.pin.isdigit():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="PIN must be 4-6 digits")

    user.pin_hash = pwd_context.hash(data.pin)
    reset_token.used = True
    db.commit()

    return {"status": "PIN reset successfully"}

@router.put("/update-profile")
async def update_profile_contact(
    data: UpdateProfileRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    if len(data.contact) != 11 or not data.contact.startswith("09") or not data.contact.isdigit():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Contact must be 11 digits starting with 09")

    current_user.contact = encrypt(data.contact)
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "updated", "contact": data.contact}from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query

router = APIRouter()


class CropTrackerRequest(BaseModel):
    crop_name: str
    date_planted: Optional[date] = None
    area_hectares: Optional[float] = 0
    growth_stage: Optional[str] = "Seedling"
    expected_harvest: Optional[date] = None
    status: Optional[str] = "active"
    yield_amount: Optional[float] = None
    yield_unit: Optional[str] = "bags"

    @field_validator('yield_unit')
    def yield_unit_valid(cls, v):
        if v is None:
            return v
        valid_units = ['bags', 'tons', 'kg', 'cavan']
        if v not in valid_units:
            raise ValueError(f'Yield unit must be one of: {valid_units}')
        return v

    @field_validator('crop_name')
    def crop_name_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Crop name cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('growth_stage')
    def growth_stage_valid(cls, v):
        valid_stages = ['Seedling', 'Growing', 'Harvesting', 'Harvested']
        if v not in valid_stages:
            raise ValueError(f'Growth stage must be one of: {valid_stages}')
        return v

    @field_validator('area_hectares')
    def area_must_be_positive(cls, v):
        if v < 0:
            raise ValueError('Area must be 0 or greater')
        return v


def get_growth_info(crop_name, date_planted):
    if not date_planted:
        return "Seedling", 0
    
    days = (date.today() - date_planted).days
    crop_lower = crop_name.lower()
    
    if any(x in crop_lower for x in ['rice', 'bigas', 'palay']):
        total_days = 120
        stages = [(20, 'Seedling'), (60, 'Growing'), (90, 'Flowering'), (120, 'Harvest Ready')]
    elif any(x in crop_lower for x in ['corn', 'mais']):
        total_days = 90
        stages = [(15, 'Seedling'), (45, 'Growing'), (70, 'Flowering'), (90, 'Harvest Ready')]
    elif any(x in crop_lower for x in ['vegetable', 'kamote', 'kangkong', 'pechay', 'sitaw', 'ampalaya']):
        total_days = 45
        stages = [(10, 'Seedling'), (25, 'Growing'), (35, 'Flowering'), (45, 'Harvest Ready')]
    else:
        total_days = 90
        stages = [(20, 'Seedling'), (50, 'Growing'), (75, 'Flowering'), (90, 'Harvest Ready')]
    
    stage = 'Harvest Ready'
    for day_limit, stage_name in stages:
        if days <= day_limit:
            stage = stage_name
            break
    
    progress = min(int((days / total_days) * 100), 100)
    return stage, progress


@router.get("/")
def get_crops(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100), # ISSUE 7 FIX: Capped pagination
    status: Optional[str] = None,
    farmer_id: Optional[int] = None
):
    try:
        query = db.query(CropTracker)
        if current_user.role == "farmer":
            query = query.filter(CropTracker.farmer_id == current_user.id)
        elif farmer_id:
            query = query.filter(CropTracker.farmer_id == farmer_id)
        if status:
            query = query.filter(CropTracker.status == status)
            
        total = query.count()
        crops = query.order_by(CropTracker.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
        
        processed_crops = []
        for c in crops:
            stage, progress = get_growth_info(c.crop_name, c.date_planted)
            # Convert to dict and update fields
            crop_dict = {
                "id": c.id,
                "farmer_id": c.farmer_id,
                "crop_name": c.crop_name,
                "date_planted": c.date_planted,
                "area_hectares": c.area_hectares,
                "growth_stage": stage,
                "progress_percent": progress,
                "expected_harvest": c.expected_harvest,
                "status": c.status,
                "yield_amount": c.yield_amount,
                "yield_unit": c.yield_unit,
                "created_at": c.created_at
            }
            processed_crops.append(crop_dict)
            
        return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": processed_crops}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch crops: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/harvest-reminders")
def get_harvest_reminders(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    days_ahead: int = Query(default=7, ge=1, le=30)
):
    """
    Returns crops whose expected harvest date is within the next X days.
    Farmers see only their own. Admins see all.
    """
    try:
        from datetime import date, timedelta
        today = date.today()
        threshold = today + timedelta(days=days_ahead)

        query = db.query(CropTracker).filter(
            CropTracker.expected_harvest >= today,
            CropTracker.expected_harvest <= threshold,
            CropTracker.status == "active"
        )

        if current_user.role == "farmer":
            query = query.filter(CropTracker.farmer_id == current_user.id)

        reminders = query.order_by(CropTracker.expected_harvest.asc()).all()
        return {
            "total": len(reminders),
            "days_ahead": days_ahead,
            "data": reminders
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch harvest reminders: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/{id}")
def get_crop(
    id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        crop = db.query(CropTracker).filter(CropTracker.id == id).first()
        if not crop:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Crop not found")
        if current_user.role == "farmer" and crop.farmer_id != current_user.id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You are not allowed to view this crop")
        
        stage, progress = get_growth_info(crop.crop_name, crop.date_planted)
        return {
            "id": crop.id,
            "farmer_id": crop.farmer_id,
            "crop_name": crop.crop_name,
            "date_planted": crop.date_planted,
            "area_hectares": crop.area_hectares,
            "growth_stage": stage,
            "progress_percent": progress,
            "expected_harvest": crop.expected_harvest,
            "status": crop.status,
            "yield_amount": crop.yield_amount,
            "yield_unit": crop.yield_unit,
            "created_at": crop.created_at
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch crop: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/")
def create_crop(
    request: CropTrackerRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    """
    Both admin and farmer can add crops
    Uses logged-in farmer's ID automatically
    """
    try:
        new_crop = CropTracker(
            farmer_id=current_user.id,
            crop_name=request.crop_name,
            date_planted=request.date_planted,
            area_hectares=request.area_hectares,
            growth_stage=request.growth_stage,
            expected_harvest=request.expected_harvest,
            status=request.status,
            yield_amount=request.yield_amount,
            yield_unit=request.yield_unit
        )
        db.add(new_crop)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(new_crop)
        log_action(db, current_user, "CREATE", "CropTracker", new_crop.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Crop added successfully", "id": new_crop.id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to add crop: {str(e)}")


@router.put("/{id}")
def update_crop(
    id: int,
    request: CropTrackerRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        crop = db.query(CropTracker).filter(CropTracker.id == id).first()
        if not crop:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Crop not found")
        if crop.farmer_id != current_user.id and current_user.role != "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You are not allowed to edit this crop")
        crop.crop_name = request.crop_name
        crop.date_planted = request.date_planted
        crop.area_hectares = request.area_hectares
        crop.growth_stage = request.growth_stage
        crop.expected_harvest = request.expected_harvest
        crop.status = request.status
        crop.yield_amount = request.yield_amount
        crop.yield_unit = request.yield_unit
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_user, "UPDATE", "CropTracker", crop.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Crop updated successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update crop: {str(e)}")

class EmergencyAlertRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str
    description: str
    alert_type: Optional[str] = "Typhoon"
    alert_level: Optional[str] = "Low"
    affected_area: Optional[str] = ""

    @field_validator('title')
    def title_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Title cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('description')
    def description_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Description cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('alert_type')
    def alert_type_valid(cls, v):
        valid_types = ['Typhoon', 'Flood', 'Pest Outbreak', 'Drought']
        if v not in valid_types:
            raise ValueError(f'Alert type must be one of: {valid_types}')
        return v

    @field_validator('alert_level')
    def alert_level_valid(cls, v):
        valid_levels = ['Low', 'Medium', 'High', 'Critical']
        if v not in valid_levels:
            raise ValueError(f'Alert level must be one of: {valid_levels}')
        return v

@router.post("/")
def create_alert(
    request: EmergencyAlertRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        new_alert = EmergencyAlert(
            title=request.title,
            description=request.description,
            alert_type=request.alert_type,
            alert_level=request.alert_level,
            affected_area=request.affected_area,
            created_by=current_admin.id
        )
        db.add(new_alert)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(new_alert)

        # Send FCM push to every farmer with a registered token
        from backend.config.firebase_config import send_push_to_token
        farmers = db.query(User).filter(User.fcm_token != None, User.fcm_token != "").all()
        notif_title = f"⚠️ EMERGENCY: {request.title}"
        notif_body = f"{request.alert_type} ({request.alert_level}): {request.description}"
        sent = 0
        for farmer in farmers:
            try:
                raw_token = safe_decrypt_fcm_token(farmer.fcm_token)  # FIX: token might be encrypted or plain
                if not raw_token:
                    continue
                result = send_push_to_token(
                    title=notif_title,
                    body=notif_body,
                    token=raw_token
                )
                if result:
                    sent += 1
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Push to farmer {farmer.id} failed: {e}")

        print(f"Push notifications sent to {sent}/{len(farmers)} farmers")
        log_action(db, current_admin, "CREATE", "EmergencyAlert", new_alert.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": f"Emergency alert created. Notified {sent} farmer(s).", "id": new_alert.id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to create alert: {str(e)}")


@router.put("/{id}")
def update_alert(
    id: int,
    request: EmergencyAlertRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        alert = db.query(EmergencyAlert).filter(EmergencyAlert.id == id).first()
        if not alert:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Alert not found")
        alert.title = request.title
        alert.description = request.description
        alert.alert_type = request.alert_type
        alert.alert_level = request.alert_level
        alert.affected_area = request.affected_area
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "UPDATE", "EmergencyAlert", alert.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Emergency alert updated successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:

class ExpenseRequest(BaseModel):
    category: Optional[str] = "Seeds"
    crop_tracker_id: Optional[int] = None
    description: Optional[str] = ""
    amount: float
    expense_type: Optional[str] = "Expense"
    date: Optional[date] = None

    @field_validator('amount')
    def amount_must_be_positive(cls, v):
        if v <= 0:
            raise ValueError('Amount must be greater than 0')
        return v

    @field_validator('expense_type')
    def expense_type_valid(cls, v):
        valid_types = ['Income', 'Expense']
        if v not in valid_types:
            raise ValueError(f'Expense type must be one of: {valid_types}')
        return v

@router.get("/farmer/{farmer_id}")
def get_farmer_expenses(
    farmer_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100), # ISSUE 7 FIX: Capped pagination
    category: Optional[str] = None,
    expense_type: Optional[str] = None
):
    try:
        if current_user.role == "farmer" and current_user.id != farmer_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You can only view your own expenses")
        query = db.query(ExpenseTracker).filter(ExpenseTracker.farmer_id == farmer_id)
        if category:
            query = query.filter(ExpenseTracker.category == category)
        if expense_type:
            query = query.filter(ExpenseTracker.expense_type == expense_type)
            
        total = query.count()
        expenses = query.order_by(ExpenseTracker.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
        return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": expenses}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch expenses: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/summary/{farmer_id}")
def get_expense_summary(
    farmer_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    try:
        if current_user.role == "farmer" and current_user.id != farmer_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You can only view your own expense summary")
        expenses = db.query(ExpenseTracker).filter(ExpenseTracker.farmer_id == farmer_id).all()
        total_income = sum(e.amount for e in expenses if e.expense_type == "Income")
        total_expense = sum(e.amount for e in expenses if e.expense_type == "Expense")
        net_profit = total_income - total_expense
        return {
            "total_income": total_income,
            "total_expenses": total_expense,
            "net_profit_loss": net_profit
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch summary: {str(e)}")

from datetime import date, timedelta
from backend.config.firebase_config import safe_decrypt_fcm_token, send_push_to_token

@router.get("/reminders/check")
async def check_calendar_reminders(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    tomorrow = (date.today() + timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
    events = db.query(FarmCalendar).filter(
        FarmCalendar.farmer_id == current_user.id,
        FarmCalendar.event_date == tomorrow
    ).all()
    return {"events": [{"id": e.id, "title": e.event_title, "event_date": str(e.event_date)} for e in events]}

@router.post("/reminders/send")
async def send_calendar_reminders(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    tomorrow = (date.today() + timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
    today_str = date.today().isoformat()
    
    events = db.query(FarmCalendar).filter(
        FarmCalendar.farmer_id == current_user.id,
        FarmCalendar.event_date == tomorrow
    ).all()

    if not events:
        return {"sent": 0}

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == current_user.id).first()
    if not user or not user.fcm_token:
        return {"sent": 0, "reason": "No FCM token"}

    raw_token = safe_decrypt_fcm_token(user.fcm_token)
    if not raw_token:
        return {"sent": 0, "reason": "Empty raw FCM token"}

    sent = 0
    for event in events:
        # Check if already notified today for this event
        cache_key = f"reminder_notified_{current_user.id}_{event.id}_{today_str}"
        already_sent = db.query(AuditLog).filter(
            AuditLog.action == cache_key
        ).first()
        
        if already_sent:
            continue

        try:
            response = send_push_to_token(
                title="📅 Farm Task Reminder",
                body=f"Tomorrow: {event.event_title} is due!",
                token=raw_token
            )
            if response:
                sent += 1
                # Mark as sent in audit log
                log = AuditLog(
                    user_id=current_user.id,
                    action=cache_key,
                    resource="FarmCalendar",
                    resource_id=event.id
                )
                db.add(log)
                db.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"FCM error: {e}")

    return {"sent": sent}from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query

class FarmingGuideRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str
    category: Optional[str] = "General"
    description: Optional[str] = ""
    content: Optional[str] = ""
    image_url: Optional[str] = None

    @field_validator('title')
    def title_not_empty(cls, v):
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Title cannot be empty')
        return v.strip()

    @field_validator('category')
    def category_valid(cls, v):
        valid_categories = ['Rice', 'Corn', 'Vegetables', 'Organic', 'General']
        if v not in valid_categories:
            raise ValueError(f'Category must be one of: {valid_categories}')
        return v


@router.get("/")
def get_guides(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    skip: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100), # ISSUE 7 FIX: Capped pagination
    category: Optional[str] = None
):
    try:
        query = db.query(FarmingGuide)
        if category:
            query = query.filter(FarmingGuide.category == category)
            
        total = query.count()
        guides = query.order_by(FarmingGuide.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
        return {"total": total, "skip": skip, "limit": limit, "data": guides}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to fetch guides: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/{id}")
def get_guide(
    id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)

    try:
        guide = db.query(FarmingGuide).filter(FarmingGuide.id == id).first()

            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Guide not found")

        raise

@router.post("/")
def create_guide(
    request: FarmingGuideRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        new_guide = FarmingGuide(
            title=request.title,
            category=request.category,
            description=request.description,
            content=request.content,
            image_url=request.image_url,
            created_by=current_admin.id
        )
        db.add(new_guide)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(new_guide)

        # Send FCM push to every farmer with a registered token (FIX: was broken topic-based)
        notif_title = f"\U0001F4DA New Farming Guide: {request.title}"
        notif_body = f"Category: {request.category} - Check it out now!"
        farmers = db.query(User).filter(User.fcm_token != None, User.fcm_token != "").all()
        sent = 0
        for farmer in farmers:
            try:
                raw_token = safe_decrypt_fcm_token(farmer.fcm_token)  # FIX: token might be encrypted or plain
                if not raw_token:
                    continue
                result = send_push_to_token(
                    title=notif_title,
                    body=notif_body,
                    token=raw_token
                )
                if result:
                    sent += 1
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Push to farmer {farmer.id} failed: {e}")
        print(f"Farming guide push sent to {sent}/{len(farmers)} farmers")

        log_action(db, current_admin, "CREATE", "FarmingGuide", new_guide.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": f"Farming guide created. Notified {sent} farmer(s).", "id": new_guide.id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to create guide: {str(e)}")


@router.put("/{id}")
def update_guide(
    id: int,
    request: FarmingGuideRequest,
    fastapi_req: Request,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_admin: User = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    try:
        guide = db.query(FarmingGuide).filter(FarmingGuide.id == id).first()
        if not guide:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Guide not found")
        guide.title = request.title
        guide.category = request.category
        guide.description = request.description
        guide.content = request.content
        if request.image_url is not None:
            guide.image_url = request.image_url
        db.commit()
        log_action(db, current_admin, "UPDATE", "FarmingGuide", guide.id, request=fastapi_req)
        return {"message": "Farming guide updated successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to update guide: {str(e)}")


@router.delete("/{id}")
def delete_guide(

    fastapi_req: Request,

):
