            const expResJson = await expRes.json();
            const expenses = expResJson.data || expResJson;
            localStorage.setItem('cache_expenses', JSON.stringify(expenses));
            cachedData.expenses = expenses;
        }

        renderFarmData(cachedData.crops || [], cachedData.calendar || [], cachedData.expenses || []);
    } catch (err) {
        // Fallback to cache on error
        const crops = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_crops') || '[]');
        const calendar = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_calendar') || '[]');
        const expenses = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_expenses') || '[]');
        renderFarmData(crops, calendar, expenses);
    }
}

function showAllItems(type) {
    let items = [];
    let title = '';
    let html = '';

    if (type === 'crops') {
        items = cachedData.crops || [];
        title = '🌾 All Crops';
        html = items.map((c, idx) => `
            <div onclick="event.stopPropagation(); viewDetail('crop', ${idx})" style="padding:12px; background:#f8fafc; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:10px; cursor:pointer;">
                <p style="font-weight:700; margin:0;">${c.crop_name}</p>
                ${c.progress_percent >= 100
                ? `<button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); showConfirmModal('Mark this crop as harvested? This will remove it from your active crops.', () => deleteEntry('crop', ${c.id}))" style="width:100%; padding:8px; background:#1b5e35; color:white; border:none; border-radius:10px; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer; margin-top:6px;">🌾 Ready to Harvest</button>`
                : `<div class="progress-container"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:${c.progress_percent || 0}%"></div></div><p style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-muted);">${c.growth_stage || 'Seedling'} • ${c.progress_percent || 0}% complete</p>`
            }
            </div>`).join('') || '<p>No crops.</p>';
    } else if (type === 'calendar') {
        items = cachedData.calendar || [];
        title = '📅 All Tasks';
        html = items.map((e, idx) => {
            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            const isCurrentOrFuture = e.event_date && e.event_date <= today;
            return `
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#f8fafc; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:10px;">
                <div onclick="event.stopPropagation(); viewDetail('event', ${idx})" style="cursor:pointer;">
                    <p style="font-weight:700; margin:0;">${e.title || e.event_title}</p>
                    <p style="font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--text-muted);">${e.event_date || ''} • ${e.event_type || ''}</p>
                </div>
                ${isCurrentOrFuture ? `<button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); showConfirmModal('Mark this task as done? This will remove it.', () => deleteEntry('event', ${e.id}))" style="width:100%; padding:6px; background:#1b5e35; color:white; border:none; border-radius:8px; font-size:0.78rem; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer; margin-top:5px;">✓ Mark as Done</button>` : ''}
            </div>`;
        }).join('') || '<p>No tasks.</p>';
    } else if (type === 'expenses') {
        items = cachedData.expenses || [];
        title = '💰 All Expenses';
        html = items.map((ex, idx) => `
            <div onclick="event.stopPropagation(); viewDetail('expense', ${idx})" style="padding:12px; background:#f8fafc; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:10px; cursor:pointer;">
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between;">
                    <p style="font-weight:700; margin:0;">${ex.category}</p>
                    <p style="font-weight:700; color:#1b5e35; margin:0;">₱${(ex.amount || 0).toFixed(2)}</p>
                </div>
                <p style="font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--text-muted);">${ex.description || ''} • ${ex.expense_type || ''}</p>
            </div>`).join('') || '<p>No expenses.</p>';
    }

    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.style.cssText = 'position:fixed; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index:99999; display:flex; align-items:flex-end;';
    modal.innerHTML = `
        <div style="background:white; width:100%; max-height:80vh; border-radius:20px 20px 0 0; padding:20px; overflow-y:auto;">
            <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:15px;">
                <h3 style="margin:0;">${title}</h3>
                <button onclick="this.closest('div[style*=fixed]').remove()" style="background:none; border:none; font-size:1.5rem; cursor:pointer;">✕</button>
            </div>
            ${html}
        </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
}


async function pestRiskPrediction() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-analyze-risk');
    const resultArea = document.getElementById('ml-result-area');
    const resultBox = document.getElementById('ml-result-box');

    if (!btn) return;

    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Analyzing...';

    try {
        // 1. Get Crop Context
        let crops = [];
        try {
            const cropRes = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/crop-tracker/`);
            if (cropRes) {
                const cropData = await cropRes.json();
                crops = cropData.data || cropData || [];
            }
        } catch (cropErr) {
            console.warn("Failed to fetch crop context:", cropErr);
            crops = [];
        }

        // 2. Get Geolocation
        let lat = 14.2772, lon = 121.4125; // Default Santa Cruz
        try {
            const pos = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(resolve, reject, { timeout: 10000 });
            });
            lat = pos.coords.latitude;
            lon = pos.coords.longitude;
        } catch (geoErr) {
            console.warn("Geolocation failed, using default:", geoErr);
        }

        // 3. Fetch Weather
        const weatherRes = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/weather/?lat=${lat}&lon=${lon}`);
        if (!weatherRes) throw new Error("Weather fetch failed");
        const wData = await weatherRes.json();

        // Extract params from Open-Meteo structure
        const temp = wData.current.temperature_2m;
        const humidity = wData.current.relative_humidity_2m;
        const rainfall = wData.current.rain || 0;

        // 4. Call ML API for each crop
        let allResults = [];
        if (crops.length > 0) {
            for (const crop of crops) {
                try {
                    let mlUrl = `${BASE_URL}/ml/pest-risk?temperature=${temp}&humidity=${humidity}&rainfall=${rainfall}&crop_name=${encodeURIComponent(crop.crop_name)}`;
                    const mlRes = await fetchWithAuth(mlUrl);
                    if (mlRes) {
                        const mlData = await mlRes.json();
                        allResults.push(mlData);
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error("ML Error for crop", crop.crop_name, e);
                }
            }
        } else {
            try {
                let mlUrl = `${BASE_URL}/ml/pest-risk?temperature=${temp}&humidity=${humidity}&rainfall=${rainfall}`;
                const mlRes = await fetchWithAuth(mlUrl);
                if (mlRes) {
                    const mlData = await mlRes.json();
                    allResults.push(mlData);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("ML Error fallback", e);
            }
        }

        // 5. Filter High Risk
        const highRiskCrops = allResults.filter(res => res.pest_risk === 'High');

        // 6. Display results
        if (resultArea) resultArea.style.display = 'block';

        if (resultBox) {
            if (highRiskCrops.length > 0) {
                resultBox.innerHTML = highRiskCrops.map(res => `
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 20px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding-bottom: 15px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                            <span style="font-weight:700; font-size: 1rem;">🌾 ${res.crop_name || 'General'}</span>
                            <span style="padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: 20px; background: var(--danger, #dc2626); color: white; font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase;">${res.pest_risk}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                            <span style="font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: 600;">Confidence:</span>
                            <span style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--primary); font-weight: 700;">${(res.confidence * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style="margin-bottom:8px;">
                            <span style="font-weight:600; font-size: 0.85rem;">🐛 Likely Pests:</span>
                            <p style="color:#b91c1c; margin:4px 0; font-size: 0.82rem;">${(res.likely_pests || []).join(', ')}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div style="margin-bottom:8px;">
                            <span style="font-weight:600; font-size: 0.85rem;">⚠️ Action:</span>
                            <p style="margin:4px 0; font-size: 0.82rem; color:var(--text-main);">${res.action || res.recommendation || ''}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <span style="font-weight:600; font-size: 0.85rem;">🛡️ Prevention:</span>
                            <p style="margin:4px 0; font-size: 0.82rem; color:var(--text-muted);">${res.prevention || 'Maintain regular monitoring.'}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            } else {
                resultBox.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: var(--primary-light, #1b5e35);">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-shield-check" style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 10px;"></i>
                        <h4 style="margin: 0;">No crops at risk.</h4>
                        <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 5px;">All crops are currently safe.</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }

        if (resultArea) resultArea.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });

        // 7. Update Risk History
        const anyHigh = highRiskCrops.length > 0;
        const riskValue = anyHigh ? 1 : 0;
        const newEntry = { timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }), value: riskValue };
        await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/ml/pest-risk/history`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: authHeaders(),
            body: JSON.stringify(newEntry)
        });

        // 8. Update Chart
        initPestRiskChart();

    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Pest Prediction Error:", err);
        showToast('Failed to analyze pest risk. Check GPS and connection.', 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i> Analyze Risk';
    }
}

let pestRiskChart = null;
async function initPestRiskChart() {
    const ctx = document.getElementById('pestRiskHistoryChart');
    if (!ctx) return;

    // Handle empty state message
    let emptyMsg = document.getElementById('pest-risk-chart-empty');
    if (!emptyMsg) {
        emptyMsg = document.createElement('div');
        emptyMsg.id = 'pest-risk-chart-empty';
        emptyMsg.innerText = "No analysis yet. Press Analyze Risk to start.";
        emptyMsg.style.cssText = "position:absolute; top:50%; left:50%; transform:translate(-50%,-50%); text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; width:80%; pointer-events:none; z-index:1;";
        ctx.parentNode.style.position = 'relative';
        ctx.parentNode.appendChild(emptyMsg);
    }

    const res = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/ml/pest-risk/history`);
    const history = (res && res.ok) ? await res.json() : [];
    if (history.length === 0) {
        emptyMsg.style.display = 'block';
        if (pestRiskChart) {
            pestRiskChart.destroy();
            pestRiskChart = null;
        }
        return;
    }

    emptyMsg.style.display = 'none';
    if (pestRiskChart) pestRiskChart.destroy();

    pestRiskChart = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
            labels: history.map(h => h.timestamp),
            datasets: [{
                label: 'Risk Level',
                data: history.map(h => h.value),
                borderColor: '#2d6a4f',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(45, 106, 79, 0.1)',
                fill: true,
                tension: 0.4,
                pointRadius: 5,
                pointBackgroundColor: '#ffffff'
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false }
            },
            scales: {
                y: {
                    min: -0.2,
                    max: 1.2,
                    ticks: {
                        stepSize: 1,
                        callback: function (value) {
                            if (value === 0) return 'Low';
                            if (value === 1) return 'High';
                            return '';
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });
}


async function fetchProfile() {
    if (!token) return;

    function renderProfile(user) {
        const location = user.location || 'Laguna';
        const contact = user.contact || '---';
        const initial = user.name ? user.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : 'F';
        const profileView = document.getElementById('profile-view');
        if (profileView) {
            profileView.innerHTML = `
                <div class="card" style="text-align: center;">
                    <div class="profile-avatar">${initial}</div>
                    <h2 style="margin:0; font-size:1.6rem; font-weight:700;">${user.name}</h2>
                    <p style="color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:5px; margin-bottom:25px; font-size:0.95rem;">${user.email}</p>
                    
                    <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; margin-bottom:25px;">
                        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:15px; padding: 10px;">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-map-location-dot" style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 1.2rem;"></i>
                            <div style="text-align:left;">
                                <p style="margin:0; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700;">Province</p>
                                <p style="margin:0; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-main);">${location}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:15px; padding: 10px; width: 100%;">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-phone" style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 1.2rem; flex-shrink: 0;"></i>
                            <div style="text-align:left; flex-grow: 1;" id="profile-contact-container">
                                <p style="margin:0; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700;">Contact</p>
                                <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:10px; width: 100%;">
                                    <p style="margin:0; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-main);">${contact}</p>
                                    <button onclick="enableContactEdit('${contact}')" style="background:none; border:none; color:var(--primary); cursor:pointer; font-size:0.95rem; padding: 0 4px;" title="Edit Contact"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i></button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <button class="btn-primary notification-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleNotification()" style="margin-bottom:12px; ${(typeof Notification !== 'undefined' && Notification.permission === 'granted') ? 'background: #1b5e35; color: white; border: none;' : 'background: white; color: var(--primary); border: 2px solid var(--primary);'} box-shadow: none;">
                        ${(typeof Notification !== 'undefined' && Notification.permission === 'granted') ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-bell"></i> &nbsp; Notifications Enabled ✓' : '<i class="fa-regular fa-bell"></i> &nbsp; Enable Notifications'}
                    </button>
                    <button class="btn-primary" onclick="logout()" style="background: var(--danger); box-shadow: 0 8px 20px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.2);">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket"></i> &nbsp; Logout Safely
                    </button>
                </div>
            `;
        }
    }

    if (!navigator.onLine) {
        const cachedProfile = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_profile') || 'null');
        if (cachedProfile) renderProfile(cachedProfile);
        return;
    }

    try {
        const res = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/auth/profile`);
        if (res) {
            const user = await res.json();
            localStorage.setItem('farmer_location', user.location || 'Laguna');
            localStorage.setItem('cache_profile', JSON.stringify(user));
            renderProfile(user);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        const cachedProfile = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_profile') || 'null');
        if (cachedProfile) {
            renderProfile(cachedProfile);
        }
    }
}

window.enableContactEdit = function(currentContact) {
    const container = document.getElementById('profile-contact-container');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <p style="margin:0; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700;">Contact</p>
        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; margin-top:4px; width: 100%;">
            <input type="text" id="edit-contact-input" value="${currentContact === '---' ? '' : currentContact}" placeholder="09XXXXXXXXX" maxlength="11" 
                style="padding:6px 10px; border:1px solid #ccc; border-radius:8px; font-size:0.9rem; flex-grow:1; min-width:0; font-weight:600; outline:none; color:var(--text-main);">
            <button onclick="saveContactEdit()" style="background:var(--primary); border:none; color:white; padding:6px 12px; border-radius:8px; font-size:0.8rem; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer;" id="edit-contact-save-btn">Save</button>
            <button onclick="fetchProfile()" style="background:none; border:1px solid #ccc; color:#666; padding:5px 10px; border-radius:8px; font-size:0.8rem; cursor:pointer;">Cancel</button>
        </div>
    `;
    
    const input = document.getElementById('edit-contact-input');
    if (input) input.focus();
};

window.saveContactEdit = async function() {
    const input = document.getElementById('edit-contact-input');
    if (!input) return;
    
    const contact = input.value.trim();
    if (contact.length !== 11 || !contact.startsWith('09') || !/^\d+$/.test(contact)) {
        showToast('Contact must be 11 digits starting with 09.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('edit-contact-save-btn');
    if (saveBtn) {
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        saveBtn.textContent = '...';
    }
    
    try {
        const res = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/auth/update-profile`, {
            method: 'PUT',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ contact })
        });
        
        if (res && res.ok) {
            showToast('Contact updated successfully!', 'success');
            await fetchProfile();
        } else {
            const data = await res.json();
            showToast(formatError(data.detail) || 'Failed to update contact.', 'error');
            await fetchProfile();
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Server connection error.', 'error');
        await fetchProfile();
    }
};

async function fetchGuides() {
    const listDiv = document.getElementById('guides-list');
    if (!listDiv) return;

    if (!navigator.onLine) {
        cachedData.guides = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_guides') || '[]');
    } else {
        try {
            const res = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/farming-guides/`);
            if (!res) throw new Error('No response');
            const resJson = await res.json();
            cachedData.guides = resJson.data || resJson;
            localStorage.setItem('cache_guides', JSON.stringify(cachedData.guides));
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('fetchGuides failed:', err);
            cachedData.guides = cachedData.guides?.length ? cachedData.guides : JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_guides') || '[]');
        }
    }

    const count = cachedData.guides.length;
    listDiv.innerHTML = `
        <div onclick="window.location.href='guides.html'" style="cursor:pointer; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:10px 14px; background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e8f5ec,#d0eeda); border-radius:14px; margin-bottom:12px;">
            <div>
                <div style="font-weight:700; color:#0f3d20; font-size:0.95rem;">📚 ${count} Farming Guide${count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</div>
                <div style="font-size:0.78rem; color:#5a7a62; margin-top:2px;">Tap to view all tutorials</div>
            </div>
            <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right" style="color:#1b5e35; font-size:1rem;"></i>
        </div>
        ${count === 0 ? '<p style="color:var(--text-muted); padding:10px;">No guides available yet.</p>' :
            cachedData.guides.slice(0, 3).map((g, idx) => `
            <div class="guide-item" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); viewGuideDetail(${idx})">
                <div class="guide-icon"><i class="fa-solid fa-book-open"></i></div>
                <div class="guide-text" style="width:100%;">
                    <h4>${g.title}</h4>
                    <p>${g.category} • Tap to learn more</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('')}
    `;
}

function viewGuideDetail(idx) {
    const g = cachedData.guides[idx];
    if (!g) return;
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');
    title.innerText = g.title;
    body.innerHTML = `
        <p style="font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:10px;">${g.category}</p>
        ${g.image_url ? `<img src="${API_BASE_URL}${g.image_url}" style="width:100%;max-height:200px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:8px;">` : ''}
        <p style="margin-bottom:10px;">${g.description || ''}</p>
        <div style="background:#f1f5f9; padding:15px; border-radius:12px; font-size:0.9rem; line-height:1.6;">${g.content || 'No content available.'}</div>
    `;
    saveBtn.style.display = 'none';
    cancelBtn.innerText = 'Close';
    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

async function triggerInstall() {
    if (deferredPrompt) {
        deferredPrompt.prompt();
        const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
        console.log(`User response to the install prompt: ${outcome}`);
        deferredPrompt = null;
    }
}

// Add direct listeners for reliability
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('install-pwa-btn');
    if (btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', triggerInstall);
    }
});

function showConfirmModal(message, onConfirm) {
    const existing = document.getElementById('custom-confirm-modal');
    if (existing) existing.remove();

    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'custom-confirm-modal';
    modal.style.cssText = 'position:fixed; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index:999999; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;';
    modal.innerHTML = `
        <div style="background:white; border-radius:20px; padding:28px 24px; width:85%; max-width:340px; text-align:center; box-shadow:0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
            <p style="font-size:0.95rem; font-weight:600; color:#1a1a1a; margin:0 0 20px 0;">${message}</p>
            <div style="display:flex; gap:12px;">
                <button id="confirm-cancel-btn" style="flex:1; padding:12px; border-radius:12px; border:1px solid #ccc; background:white; font-size:0.9rem; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer; color:#555;">Cancel</button>
                <button id="confirm-ok-btn" style="flex:1; padding:12px; border-radius:12px; border:none; background:#1b5e35; color:white; font-size:0.9rem; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer;">Confirm</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
        modal.remove();
        onConfirm();
    };
    document.getElementById('confirm-cancel-btn').onclick = () => modal.remove();
    modal.onclick = (e) => { if (e.target === modal) modal.remove(); };
}

function showSuccessModal(message) {
    const existing = document.getElementById('custom-success-modal');
    if (existing) existing.remove();

    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'custom-success-modal';
    modal.style.cssText = 'position:fixed; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index:999999; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;';
    modal.innerHTML = `
        <div style="background:white; border-radius:20px; padding:28px 24px; width:85%; max-width:340px; text-align:center; box-shadow:0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
            <div style="font-size:2.5rem; margin-bottom:12px;">✅</div>
            <p style="font-size:0.95rem; font-weight:600; color:#1a1a1a; margin:0 0 20px 0;">${message}</p>
            <button id="success-ok-btn" style="width:100%; padding:12px; border-radius:12px; border:none; background:#1b5e35; color:white; font-size:0.9rem; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer;">OK</button>
        </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    document.getElementById('success-ok-btn').onclick = () => modal.remove();
    modal.onclick = (e) => { if (e.target === modal) modal.remove(); };
}

let editingId = null;

async function deleteEntry(type, id) {
    showConfirmModal("Are you sure you want to delete this?", async () => {
        let url = '';
        if (type === 'crop') url = `${BASE_URL}/crop-tracker/${id}`;
        else if (type === 'event') url = `${BASE_URL}/farm-calendar/${id}`;
        else if (type === 'expense') url = `${BASE_URL}/expense-tracker/${id}`;

        debugLog('deleteEntry called - type: ' + type + ' id: ' + id);
        debugLog('navigator.onLine: ' + navigator.onLine);
        debugLog('url: ' + url);

        if (!navigator.onLine) {
            debugLog('OFFLINE PATH triggered - saving to IndexedDB');
            try {
                const db = await getDB();
                const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
                const item = { method: 'DELETE', url, type, id, timestamp: new Date().getTime() };
                tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).add(item);
                tx.oncomplete = () => {
                    debugLog('IndexedDB save SUCCESS - delete queued');
                    if (type === 'event') {
                        if (!cachedData.calendar) {
                            cachedData.calendar = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_calendar') || '[]');
                        }
                        cachedData.calendar = cachedData.calendar.filter(item => item.id !== id);
                        localStorage.setItem('cache_calendar', JSON.stringify(cachedData.calendar));
                    }
                    if (type === 'crop') {
                        if (!cachedData.crops) {
                            cachedData.crops = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_crops') || '[]');
                        }
                        cachedData.crops = cachedData.crops.filter(c => c.id !== id);
                        localStorage.setItem('cache_crops', JSON.stringify(cachedData.crops));
                    }
                    if (type === 'expense') {
                        if (!cachedData.expenses) {
                            cachedData.expenses = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cache_expenses') || '[]');
                        }
                        cachedData.expenses = cachedData.expenses.filter(e => e.id !== id);
                        localStorage.setItem('cache_expenses', JSON.stringify(cachedData.expenses));
                    }
                    showToast('No signal. Delete queued — will sync when online.', 'offline');
                    closeAppModal();
                    fetchFarmStatus();
                };
                tx.onerror = (err) => {
                    debugLog('IndexedDB save FAILED: ' + err);
                };
            } catch (err) {
                debugLog('IndexedDB catch FAILED: ' + err);
                console.error("Failed to save delete to IndexedDB", err);
            }
            return;
        }

        debugLog('ONLINE PATH triggered - sending DELETE request');
        try {
            const res = await fetchWithAuth(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
            debugLog('DELETE response status: ' + (res ? res.status : 'null'));
            if (res && res.ok) { showSuccessModal("Deleted successfully."); closeAppModal(); fetchFarmStatus(); }
            else if (res) { showToast('Failed to delete.', 'error'); }
        } catch (e) {
            debugLog('DELETE fetch error: ' + e);
            console.error("Delete error:", e);
        }
    });
}

function viewDetail(type, idx) {
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');

    editingId = null;
    saveBtn.style.display = 'none';
    cancelBtn.innerText = 'Got it';

    if (type === 'emergency') {
        const item = cachedData.alerts[idx];
        title.innerText = item.title;
        body.innerHTML = `<p style="color:#b91c1c; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:10px;">LEVEL: ${item.alert_level}</p><p>${item.description}</p>`;
    } else if (type === 'pest') {
        const item = cachedData.pest[idx];
        title.innerText = item.pest_name;
        body.innerHTML = `<p style="font-weight:700; color:var(--primary);">Expert Advice:</p><p style="background:#f1f5f9; padding:15px; border-radius:12px;">${item.prevention_tips}</p>`;
    } else if (type === 'crop') {
        const item = cachedData.crops[idx];
        title.innerText = "Crop Status";
        body.innerHTML = `
            <div style="margin-bottom:20px;">
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; margin-bottom:15px;">
                    <div>
                        <p style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:4px;">Crop Type</p>
                        <p style="font-weight:700; color:var(--primary-dark); margin:0;">${item.crop_name}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div style="text-align:right;">
                        <p style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:4px;">Growth Stage</p>
                        <p style="font-weight:700; color:var(--primary-dark); margin:0;">${item.growth_stage}</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <p style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-muted); text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:8px;">Season Progress (${item.progress_percent || 0}%)</p>
                ${item.progress_percent >= 100
                ? `<button onclick="showConfirmModal('Mark this crop as harvested? This will remove it from your active crops.', () => deleteEntry('crop', ${item.id}))" style="width:100%; padding:10px; background:#1b5e35; color:white; border:none; border-radius:10px; font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer; margin-bottom:15px;">🌾 Ready to Harvest</button>`
                : `<div class="progress-container" style="height:12px; margin-bottom:15px;"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:${item.progress_percent || 0}%"></div></div>`
            }
                <div style="background:#f1f5f9; padding:12px; border-radius:12px; font-size:0.85rem;">
                    <p style="margin:0 0 5px 0;">📅 <strong>Planted:</strong> ${item.date_planted || 'Not specified'}</p>
                    <p style="margin:0;">📍 <strong>Area:</strong> ${item.area_hectares || 0} Hectares</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex; gap:10px;">
                <button class="btn-primary" onclick="openModal('crop', ${idx})" style="flex:1;">✏️ Edit Details</button>
                <button class="btn-primary" style="background:#ef4444; border:none; flex:1;" onclick="deleteEntry('crop', ${item.id})">🗑️ Delete</button>
            </div>
        `;
    } else {
        let item, id;
        if (type === 'crop') { item = cachedData.crops[idx]; id = item.id; }
        else if (type === 'event') { item = cachedData.calendar[idx]; id = item.id; }
        else if (type === 'expense') { item = cachedData.expenses[idx]; id = item.id; }

        title.innerText = "Manage Entry";
        body.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display:flex; gap:10px; flex-direction:column;">
                <button class="btn-primary" onclick="openModal('${type}', ${idx})">✏️ Edit Details</button>
                <button class="btn-primary" style="background:#ef4444; border:none;" onclick="deleteEntry('${type}', ${id})">🗑️ Delete Permanently</button>
            </div>
        `;
    }
    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function openModal(type, editIdx = null) {
    currentModalType = type;
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');
    saveBtn.style.display = 'block';
    cancelBtn.innerText = 'Cancel';
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

    if (editIdx !== null) {
        title.innerText = 'Edit Entry';
        if (type === 'crop') {
            const item = cachedData.crops[editIdx];
            editingId = item.id;
            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Crop Name</label><input type="text" id="mCropName" value="${item.crop_name}"></div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Date Planted</label>
                    <input type="date" id="mCropDatePlanted" max="${today}" value="${item.date_planted || ''}" style="width:100%; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #ccc; margin-bottom:14px;">
                </div>

            `;
        } else if (type === 'event') {
            const item = cachedData.calendar[editIdx];
            editingId = item.id;
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Task Title</label><input type="text" id="mEventTitle" value="${item.event_title}"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Task Description</label><input type="text" id="mEventDesc" value="${item.event_description}"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Due Date</label><input type="date" id="mEventDate" min="${today}" value="${item.event_date}"></div>
            `;
        } else if (type === 'expense') {
            const item = cachedData.expenses[editIdx];
            editingId = item.id;
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Category</label><select id="mExpCat"><option value="Seeds" ${item.category == 'Seeds' ? 'selected' : ''}>Seeds 🌱</option><option value="Fertilizer" ${item.category == 'Fertilizer' ? 'selected' : ''}>Fertilizer 🧪</option><option value="Labor" ${item.category == 'Labor' ? 'selected' : ''}>Labor 👷</option><option value="Equipment" ${item.category == 'Equipment' ? 'selected' : ''}>Equipment 🚜</option><option value="Pest Control" ${item.category == 'Pest Control' ? 'selected' : ''}>Pest Control 🦟</option><option value="Irrigation" ${item.category == 'Irrigation' ? 'selected' : ''}>Irrigation 💧</option><option value="Other" ${item.category == 'Other' ? 'selected' : ''}>Other 📦</option></select></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Description</label><input type="text" id="mExpDesc" value="${item.description}"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Type</label><select id="mExpType"><option value="Expense" ${item.expense_type == 'Expense' ? 'selected' : ''}>Expense</option><option value="Income" ${item.expense_type == 'Income' ? 'selected' : ''}>Income</option></select></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Amount (₱)</label><input type="number" id="mExpAmt" value="${item.amount}"></div>
            `;
        }
    } else {
        editingId = null;
        if (type === 'crop') {
            title.innerText = 'Track New Crop';
            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Crop Name</label><input type="text" id="mCropName"></div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Date Planted</label>
                    <input type="date" id="mCropDatePlanted" max="${today}" style="width:100%; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #ccc; margin-bottom:14px;">
                </div>

            `;
        } else if (type === 'event') {
            title.innerText = 'New Task';
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Task Title</label><input type="text" id="mEventTitle" placeholder="e.g. Fertilizing"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Task Description</label><input type="text" id="mEventDesc" placeholder="e.g. Use 2 bags of organic"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Due Date</label><input type="date" id="mEventDate" min="${today}"></div>
            `;
        } else if (type === 'expense') {
            title.innerText = 'Log Financial Activity';
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="form-group"><label>Category</label><select id="mExpCat" onchange="document.getElementById('otherCatGroup').style.display = this.value === 'Other' ? 'block' : 'none'; document.getElementById('mExpCatCustom').value = '';"><option value="Seeds">Seeds 🌱</option><option value="Fertilizer">Fertilizer 🧪</option><option value="Labor">Labor 👷</option><option value="Equipment">Equipment 🚜</option><option value="Pest Control">Pest Control 🦟</option><option value="Irrigation">Irrigation 💧</option><option value="Other">Other 📦</option></select></div>
                <div class="form-group" id="otherCatGroup" style="display:none;">
                    <label>Custom Category</label>
                    <input type="text" id="mExpCatCustom" placeholder="Enter custom category">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Description</label><input type="text" id="mExpDesc" placeholder="e.g. 5 bags of organic"></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Type</label><select id="mExpType"><option value="Expense">Expense</option><option value="Income">Income</option></select></div>
                <div class="form-group"><label>Amount (₱)</label><input type="number" id="mExpAmt"></div>
            `;
        }
    }
    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeAppModal() {
    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'none';
    // Reset cancel button to a clean visible state so subsequent modals always show it
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');
    if (cancelBtn) {
        cancelBtn.disabled = false;
        cancelBtn.style.opacity = '1';
        cancelBtn.style.display = '';
        cancelBtn.innerText = 'Close';
        cancelBtn.onclick = closeAppModal;
    }
    // Reset save button display so it's not stuck hidden from a read-only modal
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    if (saveBtn) {
        saveBtn.style.display = '';
        saveBtn.innerText = 'Submit';
        saveBtn.onclick = saveEntry;
    }
}

async function saveEntry() {
    let url = '', payload = {};
    if (currentModalType === 'crop') {
        const cropName = document.getElementById('mCropName').value.trim();
        if (!cropName) { showToast('Crop name cannot be empty.', 'warning'); return; }
    }
    if (currentModalType === 'event') {
        const eventTitle = document.getElementById('mEventTitle').value.trim();
        const eventDate = document.getElementById('mEventDate').value;
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        if (eventDate && eventDate < today) {
            showToast('Please select today or a future date.', 'warning');
            return;
        }
        if (!eventTitle) { showToast('Event title cannot be empty.', 'warning'); return; }
        if (!eventDate) { showToast('Event date cannot be empty.', 'warning'); return; }
    }
    if (currentModalType === 'expense') {
        const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('mExpAmt').value);
        if (!amount || amount <= 0) { showToast('Amount must be greater than 0.', 'warning'); return; }
    }

    if (currentModalType === 'crop') {
        url = `${BASE_URL}/crop-tracker/`;
        if (editingId) url += editingId;
        payload = {
            crop_name: document.getElementById('mCropName').value,
            growth_stage: 'Seedling',
            date_planted: document.getElementById('mCropDatePlanted').value || null
        };
    } else if (currentModalType === 'event') {
        url = `${BASE_URL}/farm-calendar/`;
        if (editingId) url += editingId;
        payload = {
            event_title: document.getElementById('mEventTitle').value,
            event_description: document.getElementById('mEventDesc').value,
            event_date: document.getElementById('mEventDate').value
        };
    } else if (currentModalType === 'expense') {
        url = `${BASE_URL}/expense-tracker/`;
        if (editingId) url += editingId;
        payload = {
            category: document.getElementById('mExpCat').value === 'Other' && document.getElementById('mExpCatCustom') && document.getElementById('mExpCatCustom').value.trim() ? document.getElementById('mExpCatCustom').value.trim() : document.getElementById('mExpCat').value,
            description: document.getElementById('mExpDesc').value,
            amount: parseFloat(document.getElementById('mExpAmt').value),
            expense_type: document.getElementById('mExpType') ? document.getElementById('mExpType').value : 'Expense'
        };
    }

    await handleOfflineSave(url, payload, true);

    // Optimistically add to cachedData and localStorage so it shows immediately
    if (currentModalType === 'crop') {
        const newCrop = { ...payload, id: Date.now(), progress_percent: 0, growth_stage: 'Seedling' };
        cachedData.crops = cachedData.crops || [];
        if (editingId) {
            cachedData.crops = cachedData.crops.map(c => c.id === editingId ? { ...c, ...payload } : c);
        } else {
            cachedData.crops.push(newCrop);
        }
        localStorage.setItem('cache_crops', JSON.stringify(cachedData.crops));
    } else if (currentModalType === 'event') {
        const newEvent = { ...payload, id: Date.now() };
        cachedData.calendar = cachedData.calendar || [];
        if (editingId) {
            cachedData.calendar = cachedData.calendar.map(e => e.id === editingId ? { ...e, ...payload } : e);
        } else {
            cachedData.calendar.push(newEvent);
        }
        localStorage.setItem('cache_calendar', JSON.stringify(cachedData.calendar));
    } else if (currentModalType === 'expense') {
        const newExpense = { ...payload, id: Date.now(), created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        cachedData.expenses = cachedData.expenses || [];
        if (editingId) {
            cachedData.expenses = cachedData.expenses.map(e => e.id === editingId ? { ...e, ...payload } : e);
        } else {
            cachedData.expenses.push(newExpense);
        }
        localStorage.setItem('cache_expenses', JSON.stringify(cachedData.expenses));
    }

    closeAppModal();
    fetchFarmStatus();
    editingId = null;
    if (navigator.onLine) {
        syncOfflineData();
    }
}

// IndexedDB Helper for Offline Sync (ISSUE 4 FIX)
const DB_NAME = 'agriassist_offline';
const DB_VERSION = 1;
const STORE_NAME = 'sync_queue';

function getDB() {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
        request.onupgradeneeded = (e) => {
            const db = e.target.result;
            if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
                db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
            }
        };
    });
}

async function handleOfflineSave(url, payload, silent = false) {
    const method = editingId ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
    const item = { url, payload, type: currentModalType, method: method, timestamp: new Date().getTime() };

    try {
        const db = await getDB();
        const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
        tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).add(item);

        tx.oncomplete = () => {
            if (!silent && !navigator.onLine) {
                showToast("No signal. Saved to offline drafts — will sync when online.", 'offline');
            }
        };
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to save to IndexedDB", err);
    }
}

async function syncOfflineData() {
    if (!navigator.onLine) return;

    try {
        const db = await getDB();
        const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly');
        const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
        const request = store.getAll();

        request.onsuccess = async () => {
            try {
                const queue = request.result;
                if (queue.length === 0) return;

                let syncedCount = 0;
                console.log(`Attempting to sync ${queue.length} items...`);

                for (const item of queue) {
                    try {
                        const method = item.method || 'POST';

                        if (method === 'DELETE') {
                            const res = await fetchWithAuth(item.url, { method: 'DELETE' });
                            if (res && res.ok) {
                                const delTx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
                                delTx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).delete(item.id);
                                syncedCount++;
                            }
                            continue;
                        }

                        let shouldSync = true;

                        if (method === 'PUT') {
                            const checkRes = await fetchWithAuth(item.url, { method: 'GET' });
                            if (checkRes && checkRes.status === 404) {
                                shouldSync = false; // Record gone, skip and delete from queue
                            } else if (checkRes && checkRes.ok) {
                                const serverData = await checkRes.json();
                                const record = serverData.data || serverData;
                                const serverTimeStr = record.updated_at || record.created_at;
                                if (serverTimeStr && new Date(serverTimeStr).getTime() > item.timestamp) {
                                    shouldSync = false; // Server version newer, skip and delete
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if (shouldSync) {
                            const res = await fetchWithAuth(item.url, {
                                method: method,
                                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                                body: JSON.stringify(item.payload)
                            });

                            if (res && res.ok) {
                                const delTx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
                                delTx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).delete(item.id);
                                syncedCount++;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // Skip logic: still delete from queue as it shouldn't be retried
                            const delTx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
                            delTx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).delete(item.id);
                        }
                    } catch (err) {
                        console.error("Sync failed for item - will retry later", item);
                    }
                }

                if (syncedCount > 0) {
                    fetchFarmStatus();
                    showToast(`Sync complete! ${syncedCount} items uploaded.`, 'success');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("syncOfflineData onsuccess error:", err);
            }
        };
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("IndexedDB sync error:", err);
    }
}

// ISSUE 8 FIX: Offline Banner Logic
function updateOnlineStatus() {
    let banner = document.getElementById('offline-status-banner');
    if (!navigator.onLine) {
        if (!banner) {
            banner = document.createElement('div');
            banner.id = 'offline-status-banner';
            banner.style = "position:fixed; top:0; left:0; width:100%; background:#ef4444; color:white; text-align:center; padding:10px; font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:700; z-index:9999;";
            banner.innerHTML = "📡 YOU ARE OFFLINE - Using Local Data Fallback";
            document.body.prepend(banner);
        }
    } else {
        if (banner) banner.remove();
        syncOfflineData();
    }
}

window.addEventListener('online', updateOnlineStatus);
window.addEventListener('offline', updateOnlineStatus);

function logout() {
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');

    title.innerText = "Confirm Logout";
    title.style.color = "var(--primary)";
    
    body.innerHTML = `
        <p style="font-size:1.05rem; color:var(--text-main); text-align:center; margin:20px 0;">
            Are you sure you want to logout?
        </p>
    `;

    saveBtn.style.display = 'block';
    saveBtn.innerText = "Yes, Logout";
    saveBtn.className = "btn-primary";
    saveBtn.style.background = ""; // Reset in case other modals modified it

    cancelBtn.innerText = "Cancel";
    cancelBtn.disabled = false;
    cancelBtn.style.opacity = "1";

    saveBtn.onclick = () => {
        closeAppModal();
        handleLogout();
    };

    cancelBtn.onclick = () => {
        closeAppModal();
    };

    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}



function showOnboardingModal() {
    const steps = [
        { title: "Welcome to AgriAssist!", body: "Step 1: Track your crops and growth stages in the <b>Farm</b> tab." },
        { title: "Stay Alert", body: "Step 2: Check for emergency and pest alerts in the <b>Alerts</b> tab to protect your harvest." },
        { title: "Learn & Grow", body: "Step 3: Read expert farming guides in the <b>Guides</b> tab to improve your yields." }
    ];
    let currentStep = 0;

    const modal = document.getElementById('app-modal');
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');

    function updateOnboarding() {
        title.innerText = steps[currentStep].title;
        body.innerHTML = `<p style="padding:20px; text-align:center; font-size:1.1rem;">${steps[currentStep].body}</p>`;
        saveBtn.innerText = currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? "Done" : "Next";
        cancelBtn.innerText = "Skip";
    }

    saveBtn.onclick = () => {
        if (currentStep < steps.length - 1) {
            currentStep++;
            updateOnboarding();
        } else {
            localStorage.setItem('onboarding_complete', 'true');
            closeAppModal();
        }
    };

    cancelBtn.onclick = () => {
        localStorage.setItem('onboarding_complete', 'true');
        closeAppModal();
    };

    updateOnboarding();
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
}

async function sendOTP() {
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/auth/send-otp`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: authHeaders()
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            showToast(formatError(data.detail), 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Failed to send OTP. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

function showOTPModal() {
    const userEmail = document.getElementById('loginEmail')?.value || '';
    const title = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('modal-save-btn');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('modal-cancel-btn');

    title.innerText = "OTP Verification";
    body.innerHTML = `
        <p style="font-size:0.9rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:20px;">
            A 6-digit code has been sent to your email. Please enter it below to verify your login.
        </p>
        <div class="form-group">
            <label>Verification Code</label>
            <input type="text" id="otpCode" maxlength="6" placeholder="123456" style="text-align:center; font-size:1.5rem; letter-spacing:5px; font-weight:700;">
        </div>
        <div id="otp-timer" style="text-align:center; margin-top:10px; font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--primary);">
            Resend in 5:00
        </div>
    `;

    saveBtn.innerText = "Verify OTP";
    cancelBtn.innerText = "Resend OTP";
    cancelBtn.disabled = true;
    cancelBtn.style.opacity = "0.5";

    let timeLeft = 300;
    const timerInterval = setInterval(() => {
        timeLeft--;
        if (timeLeft <= 0) {
            clearInterval(timerInterval);
            const timerEl = document.getElementById('otp-timer');
            if (timerEl) timerEl.innerText = "";
            cancelBtn.disabled = false;
            cancelBtn.style.opacity = "1";
        } else {
            const timerEl = document.getElementById('otp-timer');
            if (timerEl) {
                const mins = Math.floor(timeLeft / 60);
                const secs = timeLeft % 60;
                timerEl.innerText = `Resend in ${mins}:${secs < 10 ? '0' : ''}${secs}`;
            }
        }
    }, 1000);

    saveBtn.onclick = async () => {
        const otp_code = document.getElementById('otpCode').value;
        if (otp_code.length !== 6) { showToast('Please enter a 6-digit code.', 'warning'); return; }

        try {
            const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/auth/verify-otp`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: authHeaders(),
                body: JSON.stringify({ otp_code })
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (res.ok && data.verified) {
                // Remember this device for 30 days
                const trustedKey = 'trusted_device_' + userEmail;
                const thirtyDays = new Date().getTime() + (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
                localStorage.setItem(trustedKey, thirtyDays.toString());

                clearInterval(timerInterval);
                closeAppModal();
                // Check if PIN is set, if not ask to create one
                const pinRes = await fetchWithAuth(`${BASE_URL}/auth/has-pin`);
                const pinData = await pinRes.json();
                if (!pinData.has_pin) {
                    showSetPinModal();
                } else {
                    showPinModal(document.getElementById('loginEmail')?.value || '');
                }
                requestNotificationPermission();
            } else {
                showToast(formatError(data.detail) || 'Invalid or expired OTP.', 'error');
            }
        } catch (err) {
            showToast('Verification failed. Please try again.', 'error');
        }
    };

    cancelBtn.onclick = async () => {
        clearInterval(timerInterval);
        await sendOTP();
        showOTPModal(); // Restart modal with timer
    };

    document.getElementById('app-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}
window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
    if (token) {
        handleLogout();
    }
});

document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    if (!document.hidden && token) {
        handleLogout();
    }
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem('fresh_login');
    initApp();
});

    <header>
        <button class="back-btn" onclick="history.back()"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i></button>
        <h1>Announcements</h1>
    </header>

    <div class="container" id="announce-container">
        <div id="loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> Checking for updates...</div>
    </div>

    <!-- Detail Modal -->
    <div id="detail-modal" onclick="if(event.target === this) closeModal()">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <button class="close-modal" onclick="closeModal()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
            <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
            <div id="modal-body"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function setCookie(name, value, days) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + ";expires=" + d.toUTCString() + ";path=/;SameSite=Lax";
        }
        function getCookie(name) {
            const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
            return match ? match[2] : null;
        }
        function deleteCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC;path=/;";
        }

        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname === '')
            ? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;

        // One-time migration: carry over token from localStorage to cookie
        if (!getCookie('farmer_token')) {
            const lsToken = localStorage.getItem('farmer_token');
            if (lsToken) setCookie('farmer_token', lsToken, 7);
        }
        const token = getCookie('farmer_token');
        let currentAnnouncements = [];

        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function openModal(title, bodyHtml) {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = title;
            document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function viewAnnouncement(idx) {
            const a = currentAnnouncements[idx];
            const dateStr = new Date(a.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
            });

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-day" style="color: var(--primary);"></i> Published on ${dateStr}
                </div>
                <div style="background: #f8fafc; padding: 20px; border-radius: 16px; border-left: 4px solid var(--primary);">
                    <p style="margin: 0; white-space: pre-wrap;">${a.message}</p>
                </div>
            `;
            openModal(a.title, bodyHtml);
        }

        async function fetchAnnouncements() {
            const container = document.getElementById('announce-container');

            function renderAnnouncements(data) {
                const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                currentAnnouncements = data;
                if (!currentAnnouncements || currentAnnouncements.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-bullhorn"></i>
                            <p>No announcements at this time.</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }

                container.innerHTML = currentAnnouncements.map((a, idx) => {
                    const dateStr = new Date(a.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
                    });

                    return `
                        <div class="announce-card" onclick="viewAnnouncement(${idx})">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <span class="badge">${a.category || 'NEWS'}</span>
                                <span class="date-text"><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-day"></i> ${dateStr}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="announce-title">${a.title}</div>
                            <p class="announce-message">${a.message}</p>
                            <div class="card-footer">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            }

            // Show cached data immediately so spinner never stays stuck on app resume
            const cachedImmediate = localStorage.getItem('cache_announcements');
            if (cachedImmediate) renderAnnouncements(JSON.parse(cachedImmediate));

            if (!navigator.onLine) {
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-signal" style="opacity: 0.3; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 3rem; display: block;"></i>
                            <p>You are offline and no cached data is available.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
                return;
            }

            try {
                const controller = new AbortController();
                const fetchTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/announcements/`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(fetchTimeout);

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load announcements');

                const resJson = await res.json();
                const data = (resJson.data || resJson).sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
                localStorage.setItem('cache_announcements', JSON.stringify(data));
                renderAnnouncements(data);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                            <p>Failed to load announcements. Please try again.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fetchAnnouncements);
        // Re-fetch when app is brought back to foreground (fixes Android PWA resume)
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchAnnouncements();
        });
    </script>

</body>

            margin-bottom: 16px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
            border-left: 6px solid #ccc;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
            border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .alert-card::after {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            right: 0;
            width: 40px;
            height: 40px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, transparent 50%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.02) 50%);
        }

        .alert-card:active {
            transform: scale(0.98);
            box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
        }

        .card-header {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 14px;
        }

        .badge-row {
            display: flex;
            gap: 8px;
        }

        .badge {
            color: white;
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            padding: 5px 12px;
            border-radius: 20px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.8px;
            box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
        }

        .date-text {
            font-size: 0.72rem;

            font-weight: 600;
        }

        .alert-title {

        .alert-meta {

<body>
    <header>
        <button class="back-btn" onclick="history.back()"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i></button>
        <h1>Emergency Alerts</h1>
    </header>

    <div class="container" id="alert-container">
        <div id="loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> Loading updates...</div>
    </div>

    <!-- Detail Modal -->
    <div id="detail-modal" onclick="if(event.target === this) closeModal()">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <button class="close-modal" onclick="closeModal()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
            <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
            <div id="modal-body"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function setCookie(name, value, days) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + ";expires=" + d.toUTCString() + ";path=/;SameSite=Lax";
        }
        function getCookie(name) {
            const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
            return match ? match[2] : null;
        }
        function deleteCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC;path=/;";
        }

        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname === '')
            ? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;

        // One-time migration: carry over token from localStorage to cookie
        if (!getCookie('farmer_token')) {
            const lsToken = localStorage.getItem('farmer_token');
            if (lsToken) setCookie('farmer_token', lsToken, 7);
        }
        const token = getCookie('farmer_token');
        let currentAlerts = [];

        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function getLevelColor(level) {
            if (!level) return '#6b7280';
            const l = level.toLowerCase();
            if (l === 'critical') return '#dc2626';
            if (l === 'high') return '#ea580c';
            if (l === 'medium') return '#d97706';
            if (l === 'low') return '#16a34a';
            return '#6b7280';
        }

        function openModal(title, bodyHtml) {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = title;
            document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function viewAlert(idx) {
            const e = currentAlerts[idx];
            const color = getLevelColor(e.alert_level);
            const dateStr = new Date(e.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
            });

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="margin-bottom: 20px; display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <span class="badge" style="background: ${color}">${e.alert_level || 'Normal'}</span>
                    <span class="badge" style="background: #64748b">${e.alert_type || 'General'}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 15px;">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot"></i> <strong>${e.affected_area || 'Laguna'}</strong>
                </div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <i class="fa-regular fa-clock"></i> Reported on ${dateStr}
                </div>
                <div style="background: #f8fafc; padding: 20px; border-radius: 16px; border-left: 4px solid ${color};">
                    <p style="margin: 0; white-space: pre-wrap;">${e.description}</p>
                </div>
            `;
            openModal(e.title, bodyHtml);
        }

        async function fetchAlerts() {
            const container = document.getElementById('alert-container');

            function renderAlerts(data) {
                const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                currentAlerts = data;
                if (!currentAlerts || currentAlerts.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-shield-check" style="color: #16a34a;"></i>
                            <p>No emergency alerts at this time.</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }

                container.innerHTML = currentAlerts.map((e, idx) => {
                    const color = getLevelColor(e.alert_level);
                    const dateStr = new Date(e.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
                    });

                    return `
                        <div class="alert-card" style="border-left-color: ${color}" onclick="viewAlert(${idx})">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <div class="badge-row">
                                    <span class="badge" style="background: ${color}">${e.alert_level || 'Normal'}</span>
                                    <span class="badge" style="background: #64748b; opacity: 0.8;">${e.alert_type || 'General'}</span>
                                </div>
                                <span class="date-text"><i class="fa-regular fa-clock"></i> ${dateStr}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="alert-title">${e.title}</div>
                            <div class="alert-meta">
                                <span><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot"></i> <b>${e.affected_area || 'Laguna'}</b></span>
                            </div>
                            <p class="alert-description">${e.description}</p>
                            <div style="margin-top: 12px; padding-top: 12px; border-top: 1px solid #f1f5f9; font-size: 0.72rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--primary); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;">
                                View Details <span><i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            }

            // Show cached data immediately so spinner never stays stuck on app resume
            const cachedImmediate = localStorage.getItem('cache_emergency_alerts');
            if (cachedImmediate) renderAlerts(JSON.parse(cachedImmediate));

            if (!navigator.onLine) {
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-signal" style="opacity: 0.3; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 3rem; display: block;"></i>
                            <p>You are offline and no cached data is available.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
                return;
            }

            try {
                const controller = new AbortController();
                const fetchTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/emergency-alerts/`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(fetchTimeout);

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load alerts');

                const resJson = await res.json();
                const data = (resJson.data || resJson).sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
                localStorage.setItem('cache_emergency_alerts', JSON.stringify(data));
                renderAlerts(data);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                            <p>Failed to load alerts. Please try again.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fetchAlerts);
        // Re-fetch when app is brought back to foreground (fixes Android PWA resume)
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchAlerts();
        });
    </script>

</body>

        <div class="modal-content">
            <button class="close-modal" onclick="closeModal()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
            <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
            <div id="modal-body"></div>
