    <script>
        function setCookie(name, value, days) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + ";expires=" + d.toUTCString() + ";path=/;SameSite=Lax";
        }
        function getCookie(name) {
            const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
            return match ? match[2] : null;
        }
        function deleteCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC;path=/;";
        }

        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname === '')
            ? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;
        const API_BASE_URL = BASE_URL.replace('/api', '');

        // One-time migration: carry over token from localStorage to cookie
        if (!getCookie('farmer_token')) {
            const lsToken = localStorage.getItem('farmer_token');
            if (lsToken) setCookie('farmer_token', lsToken, 7);
        }
        const token = getCookie('farmer_token');
        let currentGuides = [];

        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function openModal(title, bodyHtml) {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = title;
            document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function viewGuide(idx) {
            const g = currentGuides[idx];
            const dateStr = new Date(g.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
            });

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="margin-bottom: 20px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                    <span class="badge">${g.category || 'GENERAL'}</span>
                    <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted); font-weight: 600;">Published ${dateStr}</span>
                </div>
                ${g.image_url ? `<img src="${API_BASE_URL}${g.image_url}" style="width:100%;max-height:300px;object-fit:contain;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:12px;">` : ''}
                <div style="background: #f8fafc; padding: 20px; border-radius: 16px; border-left: 4px solid var(--primary);">
                    <p style="margin: 0; white-space: pre-wrap;">${g.content || g.description || 'No content available.'}</p>
                </div>
            `;
            openModal(g.title, bodyHtml);
        }

        async function fetchGuides() {
            const container = document.getElementById('guide-container');

            function renderGuides(data) {
                const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                currentGuides = data;
                if (!currentGuides || currentGuides.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-book-open"></i>
                            <p>No farming guides available at this time.</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }

                container.innerHTML = currentGuides.map((g, idx) => {
                    const dateStr = new Date(g.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
                    });

                    return `
                        <div class="guide-card" onclick="viewGuide(${idx})">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <span class="badge">${g.category || 'GENERAL'}</span>
                                <span class="date-text"><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-day"></i> ${dateStr}</span>
                            </div>
                            ${g.image_url ? `<img src="${API_BASE_URL}${g.image_url}" style="width:100%;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:8px;">` : ''}
                            <div class="guide-title">${g.title}</div>
                            <p class="guide-content">${g.description || g.content || ''}</p>
                            <div class="card-footer">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            }

            // Show cached data immediately so spinner never stays stuck on app resume
            const cachedImmediate = localStorage.getItem('cache_guides');
            if (cachedImmediate) renderGuides(JSON.parse(cachedImmediate));

            if (!navigator.onLine) {
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-signal" style="opacity: 0.3; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 3rem; display: block;"></i>
                            <p>You are offline and no cached data is available.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
                return;
            }

            try {
                const controller = new AbortController();
                const fetchTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/farming-guides/`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(fetchTimeout);

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load guides');

                const resJson = await res.json();
                const data = (resJson.data || resJson).sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
                localStorage.setItem('cache_guides', JSON.stringify(data));
                renderGuides(data);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                            <p>Failed to load farming guides. Please try again.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fetchGuides);
        // Re-fetch when app is brought back to foreground (fixes Android PWA resume)
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchGuides();
        });
    </script>

<body>
    <header>
        <button class="back-btn" onclick="history.back()"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i></button>
        <h1>Market Prices</h1>
    </header>

    <div class="container">
        <div class="search-box">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i>
            <input type="text" id="crop-search" placeholder="Search for a crop..." oninput="filterCrops()">
        </div>

        <div id="price-container">
            <div id="loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> Analyzing market trends...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Detail Modal -->
    <div id="detail-modal" onclick="if(event.target === this) closeModal()">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <button class="close-modal" onclick="closeModal()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
            <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
            <div id="modal-body"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function setCookie(name, value, days) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + ";expires=" + d.toUTCString() + ";path=/;SameSite=Lax";
        }
        function getCookie(name) {
            const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
            return match ? match[2] : null;
        }
        function deleteCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC;path=/;";
        }

        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname === '')
            ? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;

        // One-time migration: carry over token from localStorage to cookie
        if (!getCookie('farmer_token')) {
            const lsToken = localStorage.getItem('farmer_token');
            if (lsToken) setCookie('farmer_token', lsToken, 7);
        }
        const token = getCookie('farmer_token');
        let currentPrices = [];

        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function openModal(title, bodyHtml) {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = title;
            document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function viewPrice(idx) {
            const p = currentPrices[idx];
            const dateStr = new Date(p.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
            });

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="margin-bottom: 25px;">
                    <div style="font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-muted); text-transform: uppercase; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-bottom: 5px;">Category</div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--primary);">${p.category || 'PRODUCE'}</div>
                </div>
                
                <div style="background: #fffbeb; padding: 20px; border-radius: 16px; border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); margin-bottom: 25px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #92400e; font-weight: 600;">Current Price</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: 800; color: #92400e; font-family: 'Outfit', sans-serif;">₱${(p.new_price || 0).toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="text-align: right; color: #92400e; font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.9rem;">
                        per ${p.unit || 'kg'}
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left"></i> Data last synced on ${dateStr}
                </div>
            `;
            openModal(p.crop_name, bodyHtml);
        }

        function filterCrops() {
            const query = document.getElementById('crop-search').value.toLowerCase();
            renderPrices(query);
        }

        function renderPrices(filter = '') {
            const container = document.getElementById('price-container');
            const filtered = currentPrices.filter(p => p.crop_name.toLowerCase().includes(filter));

            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i>
                        <p>No crops matching "${filter}"</p>
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = filtered.map((p, idx) => {
                const dateStr = new Date(p.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                    month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'
                });

                let trendHtml = '';
                if (p.prev_price) {
                    const diff = p.new_price - p.prev_price;
                    if (diff > 0) {
                        trendHtml = `<div class="trend-indicator trend-up"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-up"></i> ₱${diff.toFixed(2)}</div>`;
                    } else if (diff < 0) {
                        trendHtml = `<div class="trend-indicator trend-down"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-down"></i> ₱${Math.abs(diff).toFixed(2)}</div>`;
                    }
                }

                return `
                    <div class="price-card" onclick="viewPrice(${currentPrices.indexOf(p)})">
                        <div class="price-info">
                            <div class="crop-category">${p.category || 'PRODUCE'}</div>
                            <div class="crop-name">${p.crop_name}</div>
                            <div class="update-date"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left"></i> Updated ${dateStr}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="price-value">
                            <div class="current-price">₱${(p.new_price || 0).toFixed(2)}</div>
                            <div class="price-unit">per ${p.unit || 'kg'}</div>
                            ${trendHtml}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        async function fetchPrices() {
            const container = document.getElementById('price-container');

            function renderLocal(data) {
                const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                currentPrices = data;
                if (!currentPrices || currentPrices.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-chart-bar"></i>
                            <p>No market prices available at this time.</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }
                renderPrices();
            }

            // Show cached data immediately so spinner never stays stuck on app resume
            const cachedImmediate = localStorage.getItem('cache_market_prices');
            if (cachedImmediate) renderLocal(JSON.parse(cachedImmediate));

            if (!navigator.onLine) {
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-signal" style="opacity: 0.3; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 3rem; display: block;"></i>
                            <p>You are offline and no cached data is available.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
                return;
            }

            try {
                const controller = new AbortController();
                const fetchTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/market-prices/`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(fetchTimeout);

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load prices');

                const resJson = await res.json();
                const data = (resJson.data || resJson).sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
                localStorage.setItem('cache_market_prices', JSON.stringify(data));
                renderLocal(data);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                            <p>Failed to load market prices. Please try again.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fetchPrices);
        // Re-fetch when app is brought back to foreground (fixes Android PWA resume)
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchPrices();
        });
    </script>

</body>

            <button class="close-modal" onclick="closeModal()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
            <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
            <div id="modal-body"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function setCookie(name, value, days) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + ";expires=" + d.toUTCString() + ";path=/;SameSite=Lax";
        }
        function getCookie(name) {
            const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )' + name + '=([^;]+)'));
            return match ? match[2] : null;
        }
        function deleteCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC;path=/;";
        }

        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname === '')
            ? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;

        // One-time migration: carry over token from localStorage to cookie
        if (!getCookie('farmer_token')) {
            const lsToken = localStorage.getItem('farmer_token');
            if (lsToken) setCookie('farmer_token', lsToken, 7);
        }
        const token = getCookie('farmer_token');
        let currentAlerts = [];

        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function getSeverityColor(sev) {
            if (!sev) return '#16a34a';
            const s = sev.toLowerCase();
            if (s === 'high') return '#dc2626';
            if (s === 'medium') return '#d97706';
            if (s === 'low') return '#16a34a';
            return '#16a34a';
        }

        function openModal(title, bodyHtml) {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = title;
            document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function viewAlert(idx) {
            const p = currentAlerts[idx];
            const color = getSeverityColor(p.severity);
            const dateStr = new Date(p.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
            });

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <span class="badge" style="background: ${color}">${p.severity || 'Normal'}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 0.95rem;">
                    <span><i class="fa-solid fa-leaf" style="color: var(--primary);"></i> Affected Crop: <b>${p.affected_crop || 'General'}</b></span>
                    <span><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot" style="color: var(--primary);"></i> Region: ${p.affected_area || 'Laguna'}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <i class="fa-regular fa-clock"></i> Detected on ${dateStr}
                </div>
                <div style="background: #f0fdf4; padding: 20px; border-radius: 16px; border-left: 4px solid var(--primary);">
                    <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--primary-dark); margin-bottom: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; font-size: 0.75rem; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">Prevention & Mitigation</div>
                    <p style="margin: 0; white-space: pre-wrap;">${p.prevention_tips || 'No specific mitigation tips provided.'}</p>
                </div>
            `;
            openModal(p.pest_name, bodyHtml);
        }

        async function fetchAlerts() {
            const container = document.getElementById('alert-container');

            function renderAlerts(data) {
                const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                currentAlerts = data;
                if (!currentAlerts || currentAlerts.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-bug-slash" style="color: #16a34a;"></i>
                            <p>No pest alerts at this time.</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }

                container.innerHTML = currentAlerts.map((p, idx) => {
                    const color = getSeverityColor(p.severity);
                    const dateStr = new Date(p.created_at || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
                    });

                    return `
                        <div class="alert-card" style="border-left-color: ${color}" onclick="viewAlert(${idx})">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <div class="badge-row">
                                    <span class="badge" style="background: ${color}">${p.severity || 'Normal'}</span>
                                </div>
                                <span class="date-text"><i class="fa-regular fa-clock"></i> ${dateStr}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="alert-title">${p.pest_name}</div>
                            <div class="alert-meta">
                                <span><i class="fa-solid fa-leaf"></i> <b>${p.affected_crop || 'General'}</b></span>
                                <span><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot"></i> <b>${p.affected_area || 'Laguna'}</b></span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="mitigation-header">
                                View Mitigation Plan <span><i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            }

            // Show cached data immediately so spinner never stays stuck on app resume
            const cachedImmediate = localStorage.getItem('cache_pest_alerts');
            if (cachedImmediate) renderAlerts(JSON.parse(cachedImmediate));

            if (!navigator.onLine) {
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-signal" style="opacity: 0.3; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 3rem; display: block;"></i>
                            <p>You are offline and no cached data is available.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
                return;
            }

            try {
                const controller = new AbortController();
                const fetchTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/pest-alerts/`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(fetchTimeout);

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load alerts');

                const resJson = await res.json();
                const data = (resJson.data || resJson).sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
                localStorage.setItem('cache_pest_alerts', JSON.stringify(data));
                renderAlerts(data);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                if (!cachedImmediate) {
                    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
                    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                            <p>Failed to load alerts. Please try again.</p>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fetchAlerts);
        // Re-fetch when app is brought back to foreground (fixes Android PWA resume)
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchAlerts();
        });
    </script>

</body>

    <div class="reset-card">

        <form id="resetPinForm">
            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="newPin">New PIN</label>
                <div class="input-wrapper">
                    <input type="password" id="newPin" maxlength="6" placeholder="••••••" inputmode="numeric" required>
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-lock"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="confirmPin">Confirm New PIN</label>
                <div class="input-wrapper">
                    <input type="password" id="confirmPin" maxlength="6" placeholder="••••••" inputmode="numeric" required>
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <button type="submit" class="btn-submit" id="btnSubmit">Update PIN</button>
        </form>

        <a href="index.html" class="back-link">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Login
        </a>

    <div id="toast-container"></div>

    <script>
        // Extract token from URL parameters
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const token = urlParams.get('token');

        // Dynamic API URL Resolution
        const BASE_URL = (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1')
            ? 'http://localhost:8000/api'
            : `${window.location.origin}/api`;

        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            let container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
            const colors = {
                success: { bg: '#1b5e35', icon: '✅' },
                error: { bg: '#c0392b', icon: '❌' },
                info: { bg: '#2471a3', icon: 'ℹ️' },
                warning: { bg: '#b7770d', icon: '⚠️' }
            };
            const c = colors[type] || colors.info;
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = 'toast';
            toast.style.background = c.bg;
            toast.textContent = `${c.icon}  ${message}`;
            container.appendChild(toast);
            
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    toast.style.opacity = '1';
                    toast.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
                });
            });

            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.style.opacity = '0';
                toast.style.transform = 'translateY(20px)';
                setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 350);
            }, 3000);
        }

        // Validate Token Existence
        if (!token) {
            showToast('Invalid or missing security token.', 'error');
            document.getElementById('btnSubmit').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('btnSubmit').style.opacity = '0.5';
        }

        // Handle Form Submission
        document.getElementById('resetPinForm').onsubmit = async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const pin = document.getElementById('newPin').value;
            const confirm = document.getElementById('confirmPin').value;
            const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmit');

            if (pin.length < 4 || pin.length > 6 || !/^\d+$/.test(pin)) {
                showToast('PIN must be 4-6 digits only.', 'warning');
                return;
            }

            if (pin !== confirm) {
                showToast('PINs do not match.', 'warning');
                return;
            }

            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.textContent = 'Updating PIN...';

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/auth/reset-pin`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ token, pin })
                });

                if (res.ok) {
                    showToast('PIN updated successfully!', 'success');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = 'index.html';
                    }, 2000);
                } else {
                    const data = await res.json();
                    const detail = data.detail || 'Reset failed.';
                    showToast(typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail), 'error');
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.textContent = 'Update PIN';
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Server connection error.', 'error');
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = 'Update PIN';
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
